What the log actually says
Your console stops on [0] ui: http://127.0.0.1:8777 and never emits another line. That is not a random freeze — it is the exact position of a structural defect in serve():

python
log(0, "ui: http://127.0.0.1:%d" % port)
threading.Thread(target=discover_loop, daemon=True).start()
threading.Thread(target=autopilot, daemon=True).start()
threading.Thread(target=gh_worker, daemon=True).start()
webbrowser.open("http://127.0.0.1:%d" % port)   # ← main thread, before the server runs
srv.serve_forever()
Four things stack on top of each other:

The port is not accepting yet. webbrowser.open() runs on the main thread before serve_forever(). On Windows, with a cold or store-installed default browser, that call blocks for seconds to minutes. Everything you do in the tab is a connection-refused retry loop.
Discovery owns the only network slot. The moment the port binds, discover_loop fires a full pass: 12 sources, one global NET_LOCK, a 1.2 s floor gap, 45 s socket timeouts and a 90 s reader fallback. Every 429/403 stacks up to +25 s of penalty. No worker can recon while that runs, so the board stays empty and nothing logs.
db() builds a new sqlite connection and runs three PRAGMAs on every single call, then closes. log(), setp() and add_finding() each do that, all serialized behind the global LOCK with busy_timeout=60000. One slow writer blocks every log line for up to a minute.
ThreadingHTTPServer is left on HTTP/1.1 with no Connection: close, and the front end polls /api/state + /api/log every 2.5 s with no in-flight guard. Requests queue behind the browser's 6-connection cap.
The dashboard failure is separate and just as mechanical: render() is async with no try/catch, so the first rejected fetch leaves a half-painted tab and dirty=false freezes it forever; setInterval(refresh, 2500) overlaps itself; the entire #view is replaced on every state change, destroying scroll, focus and the paste textarea; v.querySelector('button') in the value tab throws outright.

Also: your log says 162 methods. The build should register 168. Six methods were lost in the last edit — selftest.py below catches that before launch instead of after.

bh.py — apply these seven patches
1. Interruptible network wait
Replace:

python
def _net_wait(host):
    gap = max(NET_GAP + NET_PENALTY[0], HOST_GAP.get(host, 0.0))
    wait = gap - (time.time() - max(NET_LAST[0], HOST_LAST.get(host, 0.0)))
    if wait > 0:
        time.sleep(wait)
with:

python
NET_DISC = threading.Event()          # set while discovery holds the slot


def _net_wait(host):
    gap = max(NET_GAP + NET_PENALTY[0], HOST_GAP.get(host, 0.0))
    end = time.time() + max(0.0, gap - (time.time() - max(NET_LAST[0],
                                                          HOST_LAST.get(host, 0.0))))
    while time.time() < end:
        if STOP.is_set():
            return
        time.sleep(min(0.2, end - time.time()))
2. One connection per thread
Replace the whole db() context manager:

python
@contextmanager
def db():
    c = sqlite3.connect(DB, timeout=60, isolation_level=None)
    c.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    c.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
    c.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL")
    c.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=60000")
    try:
        yield c
    finally:
        c.close()
with:

python
_LOCAL = threading.local()


def _conn():
    """One connection per thread. No connect/pragma churn per call."""
    c = getattr(_LOCAL, "c", None)
    if c is None:
        c = sqlite3.connect(DB, timeout=15, isolation_level=None)
        c.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        c.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        c.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL")
        c.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=15000")
        _LOCAL.c = c
    return c


@contextmanager
def db():
    yield _conn()
3. Take the file write off the log path
Replace log() entirely:

python
LOGQ = queue.Queue(maxsize=20000)


def log(pid, line):
    line = str(line)[:4000]
    ts = time.time()
    try:
        with db() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO log(pid,ts,line) VALUES(?,?,?)", (pid, ts, line))
            r = c.execute("SELECT slug FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
            slug = (r["slug"] if r else "") or ""
    except Exception:
        slug = ""
    try:
        LOGQ.put_nowait((slug, ts, line))
    except queue.Full:
        pass
    print("[%s] %s" % (pid, line), flush=True)


def log_writer():
    while not STOP.is_set():
        try:
            slug, ts, line = LOGQ.get(timeout=1.0)
        except queue.Empty:
            continue
        try:
            d = WORK / (slug or "0")
            d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with (d / "log.txt").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write("%s %s\n" % (time.strftime("%H:%M:%S", time.localtime(ts)), line))
        except Exception:
            pass


STARTED = [time.time()]


def heartbeat():
    """A visible pulse. If this stops, the process is dead, not 'busy'."""
    while not STOP.is_set():
        time.sleep(20)
        try:
            with RUN_LOCK:
                live = sorted(RUNNING.keys())
            with db() as c:
                t0 = time.time()
                c.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()
                dbms = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
            log(0, "hb: %d live, queue %d, db %dms, net penalty %.1fs, discover %s"
                % (len(live), JOBQ.qsize(), dbms, NET_PENALTY[0],
                   "on" if AUTO_DISCOVER else "off"))
        except Exception as ex:
            log(0, "hb: %s" % str(ex)[:120])
4. Boot order — server first, browser in a thread
Replace serve():

python
def serve():
    for port in PORTS:
        try:
            ThreadingHTTPServer.allow_reuse_address = True
            srv = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), H)
        except OSError:
            continue
        srv.daemon_threads = True
        log(0, "ui: http://127.0.0.1:%d" % port)
        threading.Thread(target=log_writer, daemon=True).start()
        threading.Thread(target=heartbeat, daemon=True).start()
        threading.Thread(target=discover_loop, daemon=True).start()
        threading.Thread(target=autopilot, daemon=True).start()
        threading.Thread(target=gh_worker, daemon=True).start()
        threading.Thread(target=lambda u: webbrowser.open(u),
                         args=("http://127.0.0.1:%d" % port,), daemon=True).start()
        srv.serve_forever()
        return
    log(0, "no free port")
5. Discovery yields to real work
Replace discover_loop():

python
DISCOVER_BUDGET = float(os.environ.get("BH_DISCOVER_BUDGET", "90"))


def discover_loop():
    time.sleep(8)
    while not STOP.is_set():
        if AUTO_DISCOVER and JOBQ.qsize() == 0 and not running_pids():
            NET_DISC.set()
            t0 = time.time()
            try:
                n = discover()
                log(0, "discover: %d new (%.0fs)" % (n, time.time() - t0))
                if n:
                    start_all()
            except Exception as ex:
                log(0, "discover loop error: %s" % ex)
            finally:
                NET_DISC.clear()
        for _ in range(DISCOVER_EVERY):
            if STOP.is_set():
                return
            time.sleep(1)

selftest.py (new — run this before every launch)
python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Import-time integrity gate. Catches a dangling name before the server does."""
import sys, re
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
for f in ("bh.py", "platforms.py", "ui.html"):
    if not (ROOT / f).exists():
        sys.exit("missing file: %s" % f)

try:
    import bh
except Exception as ex:
    sys.exit("import failed: %s: %s" % (type(ex).__name__, ex))

bad = []
for name, fn in bh.STEP_FN.items():
    if not callable(fn):
        bad.append("STEP_FN[%s]" % name)
for name in ("intake", "conditions", "brief", "gate", "report", "poc"):
    if name not in bh.STEP_FN:
        bad.append("missing step: " + name)
for tid, t in bh.TOOLS.items():
    if not callable(t.get("run")):
        bad.append("TOOL[%s] has no run" % tid)

routes = set(re.findall(r'u\.path == "(/api/[\w/]+)"', (ROOT / "bh.py").read_text(
    encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")))
for want in ("/api/state", "/api/log", "/api/health", "/api/findings", "/api/plan",
             "/api/readiness", "/api/contract", "/api/paste", "/api/attack"):
    if want not in routes and want != "/api/contract":
        bad.append("missing route " + want)

if len(bh.TOOLS) < 165:
    bad.append("tool count dropped to %d (expected 165+)" % len(bh.TOOLS))

ui = (ROOT / "ui.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
for needle in ("/api/state", "/api/health", "render"):
    if needle not in ui:
        bad.append("ui.html missing " + needle)

if bad:
    print("SELFTEST FAIL")
    for b in bad:
        print("  -", b)
    sys.exit(1)
print("selftest ok: %d steps, %d methods, %d routes" % (len(bh.STEP_FN), len(bh.TOOLS), len(routes)))

<div id="toast"></div>

<script>
"use strict";
const TABS = ["board","overview","value","contract","plan","findings","readiness",
              "report","methods","discovery","repairs","health"];
const CONF = {manual:"manual",manifest:"manifest",census:"census",github:"github language",
              address:"in-scope address",surface:"html surface",none:"unresolved"};
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const esc = v => (v ?? "").toString().replace(/[&<>"']/g,
  c => ({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c]));

let S = null, SEL = 0, TAB = "board", FILT = "all", TERM = "", FSEV = "all",
    FSORT = {k:"score", d:-1}, SINCE = 0, PAUSE = false, ERRS = false, BUSY = false,
    FAILS = 0, SIGHASH = "", DOCK = true;

function to(msg, bad){
  const d = document.createElement("div");
  d.className = "to" + (bad ? " bad" : "");
  d.textContent = msg;
  $("#toast").appendChild(d);
  setTimeout(() => d.remove(), 3600);
}
async function api(path, opt){
  const ctl = new AbortController();
  const t = setTimeout(() => ctl.abort(), 12000);
  try{
    const r = await fetch(path, {...(opt||{}), signal: ctl.signal});
    if (!r.ok){
      let j = null; try { j = await r.json(); } catch {}
      throw new Error((j && (j.msg || j.error)) || ("HTTP " + r.status));
    }
    return await r.json();
  } finally { clearTimeout(t); }
}
const get = p => api(p).catch(() => null);
const post = (p, b) => api(p, {method:"POST", headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json"},
  body: JSON.stringify(b||{})});

/* ── polling with in-flight guard and backoff ── */
async function poll(){
  if (BUSY) return;
  BUSY = true;
  try{
    const s = await api("/api/state");
    FAILS = 0; $("#lost").classList.remove("on"); $("#dot").classList.remove("bad");
    const h = JSON.stringify([s.tally, s.running, s.fund, s.stopped, s.projects.map(p =>
      [p.id,p.status,p.stage,p.cycle,p.attempts,p.escalation,p.readiness,p.hits,p.fund,
       p.ready,p.unjudged,p.details_ok,p.toolchain,p.live])]);
    const changed = h !== SIGHASH;
    S = s; SIGHASH = h;
    paintKpi(); paintRail(); paintHead(); paintHealth();
    if (changed) await render();
    await pullLog();
  }catch(e){
    FAILS++;
    if (FAILS >= 3){ $("#lost").classList.add("on"); $("#dot").classList.add("bad"); }
  }finally{ BUSY = false; }
}

function paintKpi(){
  const t = (S && S.tally) || {};
  const ready = (S.projects||[]).reduce((a,p) => a + (p.ready||0), 0);
  const avg = S.projects.length
    ? Math.round(S.projects.reduce((a,p) => a + (p.readiness||0), 0) / S.projects.length) : 0;
  $("#k1").textContent = S.projects.length;
  $("#k2").textContent = S.running;
  $("#k3").textContent = S.fund || 0;
  $("#k4").textContent = t.critical || 0;
  $("#k5").textContent = t.high || 0;
  $("#k6").textContent = ready;
  $("#k7").textContent = S.tools;
  $("#k8").textContent = avg + "%";
  $("#fund").classList.toggle("on", !!S.fund_only);
}

function paintHealth(){
  const c = $("#hchip");
  if (!S) { c.textContent = "—"; return; }
  const bits = [`${S.workers}w`, `${S.tools} methods`, `thr ${S.threshold}`];
  if (S.auto_discover) bits.push("discovery");
  if (S.fund_only) bits.push("FUND ONLY");
  if (S.stopped) bits.push("STOPPED");
  c.textContent = bits.join(" · ");
  c.className = S.stopped ? "bad" : (S.fund_only ? "warn" : "");
}

function pill(text, cls){ return `<span class="pill ${cls||""}">${esc(text)}</span>`; }

function railRow(p){
  const tags = [];
  tags.push(p.toolchain === "unknown" ? pill("untyped","bad") : pill(p.toolchain));
  tags.push(p.details_ok ? pill("details","rdy") : pill("no details","warn"));
  if (p.web) tags.push(pill("app","live"));
  if (p.fund) tags.push(pill(p.fund + " fund","cash"));
  if (p.type_locked) tags.push(pill("manual","mut"));
  if (p.live) tags.push(pill("live","live"));
  if (p.ready) tags.push(pill(p.ready + " ready","rdy"));
  if (p.unjudged) tags.push(pill(p.unjudged + " unjudged","warn"));
  if (p.hits) tags.push(pill(p.hits + " hit"));
  tags.push(pill("L" + p.escalation));
  tags.push(pill(p.status, p.status === "failed" ? "bad" : ""));
  return `<div class="t ${p.id === SEL ? "sel" : ""}" data-id="${p.id}">
    <div class="n">${esc(p.name)}</div>
    <div class="u">${esc(p.slug || "")}</div>
    <div class="b">${tags.join("")}</div>
    <div class="bar"><i style="width:${Math.min(100,(p.escalation/5)*100)}%"></i></div>
  </div>`;
}

function paintRail(){
  const box = $("#list");
  const keep = box.scrollTop;
  const t = TERM.toLowerCase();
  const rows = (S.projects || []).filter(p => {
    if (t && !(p.name||"").toLowerCase().includes(t) && !(p.url||"").toLowerCase().includes(t))
      return false;
    if (FILT === "live") return p.live;
    if (FILT === "cash") return p.fund > 0;
    if (FILT === "ready") return p.ready > 0;
    if (FILT === "hits") return p.hits > 0;
    if (FILT === "nodet") return !p.details_ok;
    if (FILT === "un") return p.toolchain === "unknown";
    if (FILT === "web") return p.web;
    return true;
  });
  box.innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(railRow).join("")
    : `<div class="empty">nothing matches</div>`;
  box.scrollTop = keep;
  box.querySelectorAll(".t").forEach(el => el.onclick = () => select(+el.dataset.id));
}

function cur(){ return (S && S.projects || []).find(p => p.id === SEL) || null; }

function select(id){
  SEL = id; SINCE = 0; SIGHASH = ""; $("#dlog").innerHTML = ""; render();
}

function paintHead(){
  const p = cur();
  $("#pn").textContent = p ? p.name : "no target selected";
  $("#pu").textContent = p ? p.url : "pick a target on the left, or queue one above";
  $("#dn").textContent = p ? p.name : "-";
  const a = $("#acts");
  if (!p){ a.innerHTML = ""; return; }
  a.innerHTML = `
    <button class="pri" data-a="all">attack all</button>
    <button data-a="l1">L1 static</button><button data-a="l2">L2 symbolic</button>
    <button data-a="l3">L3 dynamic</button><button data-a="l4">L4 adversarial</button>
    <button data-a="l5">L5 hammer</button>
    <button data-a="recon">recon</button>
    <button class="dan" data-a="stop">stop</button>
    <button data-a="restart">restart</button>
    <select id="rk">${(S.repair_kinds||[]).map(k =>
      `<option value="${esc(k)}">${esc(k)}</option>`).join("")}</select>
    <button data-a="repair">repair</button>
    <button class="gh" data-a="auto">auto ${p.auto ? "on" : "off"}</button>`;
  a.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => act(b.dataset.a, p));
}

async function act(kind, p){
  if (!p) return;
  try{
    if (kind === "all") { await post("/api/attack", {pid:p.id}); to("full ladder queued"); }
    else if (kind === "stop") { await post("/api/stop", {pid:p.id}); to("stopping"); }
    else if (kind === "restart") {
      if (!confirm("purge this target and requeue?")) return;
      await post("/api/restart", {pid:p.id}); SINCE = 0; $("#dlog").innerHTML = "";
      to("restarted");
    }
    else if (kind === "repair") {
      await post("/api/repair", {pid:p.id, kind: ($("#rk")||{}).value || "locked_files"});
      to("repair queued");
    }
    else if (kind === "auto") { await post("/api/auto", {pid:p.id, on: !p.auto}); }
    else if (kind === "recon") { await post("/api/attack", {pid:p.id, level: 0}); }
    else { const l = +kind.slice(1); await post("/api/attack", {pid:p.id, level: l}); }
    SIGHASH = ""; poll();
  }catch(e){ to(e.message, true); }
}

/* ── log dock ── */
async function pullLog(){
  if (PAUSE || !SEL) return;
  const rows = await get(`/api/log?pid=${SEL}&since=${SINCE}`);
  if (!rows || !rows.length) return;
  const box = $("#dlog");
  const bottom = box.scrollHeight - box.scrollTop - box.clientHeight < 40;
  const f = ($("#dfilter").value || "").toLowerCase();
  for (const r of rows){
    SINCE = r.id;
    if (f && !r.line.toLowerCase().includes(f)) continue;
    if (ERRS && !/\b(fail|error|failed|crash|timeout|denied|traceback)\b/i.test(r.line)) continue;
    const d = document.createElement("div");
    d.className = "ln";
    const cls = /\b(fail|error|crash|traceback)\b/i.test(r.line) ? "e"
      : /\b(warn|thin|skip|absent)\b/i.test(r.line) ? "w" : "";
    d.innerHTML = `<span class="ts">${new Date(r.ts*1000).toLocaleTimeString()} </span>` +
      `<span class="${cls}">${esc(r.line)}</span>`;
    box.appendChild(d);
  }
  while (box.childElementCount > 1200) box.firstChild.remove();
  if (bottom) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
}

/* ── tab shell ── */
function paintTabs(){
  $("#tabs").innerHTML = TABS.map(t =>
    `<button class="${t === TAB ? "on" : ""}" data-t="${t}">${t}</button>`).join("");
  $("#tabs").querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    TAB = b.dataset.t; paintTabs(); render();
  });
}

async function render(){
  const v = $("#view");
  const p = cur();
  paintTabs();
  try{
    if (TAB === "board") return renderBoard(v);
    if (!p){ v.innerHTML = `<div class="empty">select a target</div>`; return; }
    if (TAB === "overview") return await renderOverview(v, p);
    if (TAB === "value") return await renderValue(v, p);
    if (TAB === "contract") return await renderContract(v, p);
    if (TAB === "plan") return await renderPlan(v, p);
    if (TAB === "findings") return await renderFindings(v, p);
    if (TAB === "readiness") return await renderReadiness(v, p);
    if (TAB === "report") return await renderReport(v, p);
    if (TAB === "methods") return await renderMethods(v, p);
    if (TAB === "discovery") return await renderDiscovery(v);
    if (TAB === "repairs") return await renderRepairs(v);
    if (TAB === "health") return await renderHealth(v);
  }catch(e){
    v.innerHTML = `<div class="card bad"><b>render failed</b>
      <div class="hint mono">${esc(e.message)}</div>
      <div class="row" style="margin:9px 0 0"><button data-x="retry">retry</button></div></div>`;
    const b = v.querySelector('[data-x="retry"]');
    if (b) b.onclick = render;
  }
}

function renderBoard(v){
  const ps = S.projects || [];
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>all targets · ${ps.length}</h2>
    <p class="hint" style="margin-bottom:10px">details gate decides whether a repo may be
    downloaded. money is how many fund classes already hit.</p>` +
    (ps.length ? `<table><thead><tr><th>target</th><th>details</th><th>contract</th>
    <th>type</th><th>money</th><th>status</th><th>ready</th><th>ready %</th><th></th>
    </tr></thead><tbody>` + ps.map(x => `<tr>
      <td><b>${esc(x.name)}</b><div class="u mono">${esc(x.slug)}</div></td>
      <td>${x.details_ok ? '<span class="chip ok">yes</span>' : '<span class="chip bad">no</span>'}</td>
      <td>${x.cond_ok ? `<span class="chip ok">${x.targets_n||0}</span>` : '<span class="chip">—</span>'}</td>
      <td>${esc(x.toolchain)}${x.web ? ' <span class="chip ok">app</span>' : ''}
        <div class="hint">${esc(CONF[x.type_conf]||"")}</div></td>
      <td>${x.fund ? `<span class="chip cash">${x.fund}</span>` : "—"}</td>
      <td>${esc(x.status)}</td>
      <td class="num">${x.ready||0}</td>
      <td><span class="sbar"><i style="width:${x.readiness||0}%"></i></span>
        <span class="num hint">${x.readiness||0}%</span></td>
      <td><button data-g="${x.id}">open</button>
        <button class="pri" data-s="${x.id}">start</button></td></tr>`).join("") +
      `</tbody></table>` : `<div class="empty">no targets — queue a program url above</div>`);
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-g]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => select(+b.dataset.g));
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-s]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    const pr = (S.projects||[]).find(z => z.id === +b.dataset.s);
    SEL = pr.id; act("all", pr);
  });
}

async function renderOverview(v, p){
  const d = await get("/api/details?pid=" + p.id) || {};
  const f = d.details || {};
  const ev = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(p.type_evidence || "{}"); } catch { return {}; } })();
  v.innerHTML = `
    ${!p.details_ok ? `<div class="card warn"><b>details not fetched</b>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">No page, no contract, no scope. Nothing is
      downloaded until these exist.</div></div>` : ""}
    ${p.toolchain === "unknown" ? `<div class="card warn"><b>type unresolved</b>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">No manifest, no census, no repo language.
      Only language-agnostic methods run.</div></div>` : ""}
    ${p.unjudged ? `<div class="card warn"><b>${p.unjudged} finding(s) never judged</b>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">Run the gate before trusting the list.</div></div>` : ""}
    <div class="grid">
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.readiness||0}%</b><span>submission ready</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.fund||0}</b><span>fund findings</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.hits}</b><span>total findings</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.cond_ok ? p.targets_n : 0}</b><span>in scope</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.repairs}</b><span>repairs</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="card"><b>${esc(p.status)}</b> · stage <span class="mono">${esc(p.stage||"—")}</span>
      · L${p.escalation}/5 · cycle ${p.cycle} · attempt ${p.attempts}
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">rank ${p.rank} · cap $${p.max}
      ${p.tvl ? " · census " + (p.tvl/1e18).toFixed(4) + " ETH-eq" : ""}</div></div>
    <div class="card"><b>type: ${esc(p.toolchain)}</b>
      ${pill(CONF[p.type_conf]||"unresolved", p.type_locked ? "mut" : "")}
      ${ev.reason ? `<div class="hint" style="margin-top:6px">reason: ${esc(ev.reason)}</div>` : ""}
      ${ev.manifest ? `<div class="hint">manifest: ${esc((ev.manifest||[]).join(", "))}</div>` : ""}
      <div class="row" style="margin:9px 0 0">
        <select id="tsel">${["auto","solidity","rust","go","python","js","php","ruby","java",
          "c","web","unknown"].map(t => `<option value="${t}" ${
          (!p.type_locked && t === "auto") || (p.type_locked && t === p.toolchain)
          ? "selected" : ""}>${t}</option>`).join("")}</select>
        <button data-x="settype">set type</button></div></div>
    <div class="card ${p.status === "needs-details" ? "warn" : ""}">
      <b>detail resolution</b>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">adapter <span class="mono">${esc(f.adapter||"none")}</span>
        · tier <span class="mono">${esc(f.tier||"none")}</span>
        · assets ${(f.fields||{}).assets||0} · tiers ${(f.fields||{}).tiers||0}</div>
      ${d.reason ? `<pre class="rep" style="margin-top:7px">${esc(d.reason)}</pre>` : ""}
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:8px">Every tier failed? Paste the program text.
      Scope, tiers, PoC clauses and the submission channel are read from it.</div>
      <textarea id="paste" rows="7" style="margin-top:6px"></textarea>
      <div class="row" style="margin:7px 0 0">
        <button class="pri" data-x="paste">parse paste</button>
        <button data-x="probe">probe</button>
        <button data-x="recon">retry fetch</button></div>
      <div id="pout" class="hint mono" style="margin-top:7px"></div></div>
    <div class="row">
      <button class="pri" data-x="all">attack all</button>
      <button data-x="value">value</button><button data-x="contract">contract</button>
      <button data-x="plan">plan</button>
      <button class="dan" data-x="purge">purge data</button>
      <button data-x="restart">restart</button>
      <button data-x="locked_files">unlock git</button></div>`;
  v.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.onclick = async () => {
    const x = b.dataset.x;
    if (x === "all" || x === "restart") return act(x, p);
    if (["value","contract","plan"].includes(x)) { TAB = x; return render(); }
    if (x === "settype") {
      await post("/api/type", {pid:p.id, toolchain: $("#tsel").value}).catch(e => to(e.message, true));
      SIGHASH = ""; return poll();
    }
    if (x === "paste") {
      const t = $("#paste").value || "";
      if (t.trim().length < 80) return to("paste more text", true);
      try{
        const r = await post("/api/paste", {pid:p.id, text:t});
        to(`parsed ${r.assets} asset(s), ${r.tiers} tier(s)` + (r.ok ? "" : " — still thin"),
           !r.ok);
        SIGHASH = ""; poll();
      }catch(e){ to(e.message, true); }
      return;
    }
    if (x === "probe") {
      const r = await get("/api/probe?pid=" + p.id);
      $("#pout").innerHTML = r ? `adapter <b>${esc(r.adapter)}</b> · url ${
        r.url_ok ? "ok" : "bad: " + esc(r.url_reason)}<br>` +
        ((r.api_candidates||[]).map(esc).join("<br>") || "no api candidates") : "probe failed";
      return;
    }
    if (x === "purge") {
      if (!confirm("drop findings, log, contract and report for this target?")) return;
      await post("/api/purge", {pid:p.id}).catch(e => to(e.message, true));
      SINCE = 0; $("#dlog").innerHTML = ""; SIGHASH = ""; return poll();
    }
    await post("/api/repair", {pid:p.id, kind:x}).catch(e => to(e.message, true));
    to("repair: " + x);
  });
}

async function renderContract(v, p){
  const pol = await get("/api/conditions?pid=" + p.id);
  if (!pol || !p.cond_ok){
    v.innerHTML = `<div class="empty">no program contract parsed — run recon or paste</div>`;
    return;
  }
  const ex = pol.exclusions || {}, cons = pol.constraints || {}, poc = pol.poc || {},
        sub = pol.submit || {};
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>program contract</h2>
    <p class="hint" style="margin-bottom:10px">read from the page. every stage obeys it.</p>
    <div class="grid">
      <div class="st"><b>${esc(pol.scope_model)}</b><span>scope model</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${(pol.targets||[]).length}</b><span>targets</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b>${poc.required ? "required" : "optional"}</b><span>poc</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b>${esc(poc.framework||"any")}</b><span>framework</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="card"><b>targets</b><table><thead><tr><th>contract</th><th>address</th>
      <th>chain</th><th>kind</th></tr></thead><tbody>
      ${(pol.targets||[]).map(t => `<tr><td>${esc(t.contract||"—")}</td>
      <td class="mono">${esc(t.address)}</td><td>${esc(t.chain)}</td>
      <td>${esc(t.kind||"—")}</td></tr>`).join("") ||
      `<tr><td colspan="4" class="hint">none parsed</td></tr>`}</tbody></table></div>
    <div class="card"><b>poc requirements</b><table><tbody>
      ${Object.entries(poc).map(([k,val]) => `<tr><th>${esc(k)}</th><td>${
        val === true ? '<span class="chip ok">yes</span>'
        : val === false ? '<span class="chip">no</span>' : esc(val)}</td></tr>`).join("")}
      </tbody></table></div>
    <div class="card"><b>exclusions</b>
      ${["surfaces","non_vulns","non_submissions"].map(k => `
        <div class="hint" style="margin-top:7px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.09em">
        ${k.replace("_"," ")}</div>
        ${(ex[k]||[]).map(s => `<div class="chip bad" style="display:block">${esc(s)}</div>`)
          .join("") || '<div class="hint">—</div>'}`).join("")}</div>
    <div class="card"><b>template</b>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">${esc((pol.template||[]).join(" / ")||"—")}</div>
      <div class="hint">length: ${pol.one_page ? "one page max" : "unbounded"}</div></div>
    <div class="card"><b>submit</b><table><tbody>
      <tr><th>channel</th><td>${esc(sub.channel||"—")}</td></tr>
      <tr><th>address</th><td class="mono">${esc(sub.address||"—")}</td></tr>
      <tr><th>subject</th><td class="mono">${esc(sub.subject_fmt||"—")}</td></tr>
      </tbody></table></div>
    <div class="card"><b>severity tiers</b><table><thead><tr><th>tier</th>
      <th>definition</th><th>band</th></tr></thead><tbody>
      ${(pol.severity||[]).map(r => `<tr><td class="sev sv-${esc(r.tier)}">${esc(r.tier)}</td>
      <td>${esc(r.definition)}</td><td class="mono">${esc(r.band||"—")}</td></tr>`).join("") ||
      `<tr><td colspan="3" class="hint">none parsed — the gate blocks everything</td></tr>`}
      </tbody></table></div>
    <div class="card"><b>active constraints</b>
      ${Object.entries(cons).filter(([,v2]) => v2).map(([k]) =>
        `<span class="chip bad">${esc(k)}</span>`).join("") || '<div class="hint">none</div>'}</div>`;
}

async function renderFindings(v, p){
  const fs = await get("/api/findings?pid=" + p.id) || [];
  const counts = {};
  fs.forEach(f => counts[f.sev] = (counts[f.sev]||0) + 1);
  const fund = fs.filter(f => f.fund).length;
  let shown = FSEV === "all" ? fs : FSEV === "fund" ? fs.filter(f => f.fund)
    : fs.filter(f => f.sev === FSEV);
  shown = shown.slice().sort((a,b) => {
    const k = FSORT.k;
    const av = k === "sev" ? "critical high medium low info".indexOf(a.sev)
      : (a[k] ?? ""), bv = k === "sev" ? "critical high medium low info".indexOf(b.sev)
      : (b[k] ?? "");
    return (av > bv ? 1 : av < bv ? -1 : 0) * FSORT.d;
  });
  const chip = (id, label, n) =>
    `<button class="${FSEV === id ? "on" : ""}" data-s="${id}">${label} ${n}</button>`;
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>${fs.length} findings</h2>
    <div class="row">${chip("all","all",fs.length)}
      ${fund ? chip("fund","money",fund) : ""}
      ${["critical","high","medium","low","info"].filter(s => counts[s])
        .map(s => chip(s, s, counts[s])).join("")}</div>
    ${shown.length ? `<table><thead><tr>
      <th class="s" data-k="sev">sev</th><th class="s" data-k="title">title</th>
      <th>where</th><th class="s" data-k="score">score</th><th>band</th><th>state</th>
      </tr></thead><tbody>` + shown.map(f => {
        let bl = []; try { bl = JSON.parse(f.blockers || "[]"); } catch {}
        return `<tr class="${f.eligible ? "" : "dead"}">
          <td class="sev sv-${esc(f.sev)}">${esc(f.sev)}</td>
          <td>${f.fund ? '<span class="chip cash">$</span> ' : ""}${esc(f.title)}
            <div class="hint mono">${esc(f.cls)} · gate ${esc(f.gate)}</div></td>
          <td class="mono">${esc(f.file)}:${f.line}</td>
          <td><span class="sbar"><i style="width:${f.score||0}%"></i></span>
            <span class="num hint">${f.score||0}</span></td>
          <td class="num">${f.reward ? "$" + f.reward : "—"}</td>
          <td>${!f.judged ? '<span class="chip">unjudged</span>'
            : bl.length ? bl.map(b => `<span class="chip bad">${esc(b)}</span>`).join("")
            : '<span class="chip ok">ready</span>'}</td></tr>
          <tr><td colspan="6" style="padding-top:0">
            <details><summary class="hint">evidence</summary>
            <pre>${esc(f.evidence)}</pre>
            ${f.poc_path ? `<div class="hint mono">poc: ${esc(f.poc_path)}</div>` : ""}
            </details></td></tr>`;
      }).join("") + `</tbody></table>`
    : `<div class="empty">no findings in this filter</div>`}`;
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-s]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { FSEV = b.dataset.s; render(); });
  v.querySelectorAll("th.s").forEach(th => th.onclick = () => {
    const k = th.dataset.k;
    FSORT = {k, d: FSORT.k === k ? -FSORT.d : -1};
    render();
  });
}

async function renderReport(v, p){
  const r = await get("/api/report?pid=" + p.id);
  const txt = (r && r.report) || "";
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>report</h2>
    <div class="row"><button data-x="copy">copy</button>
      <button data-x="dl">download</button>
      <button class="pri" data-x="l5">rebuild</button></div>
    <pre class="rep">${esc(txt || "not written yet")}</pre>`;
  v.querySelector("[data-x=copy]").onclick = () =>
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(() => to("copied"), () => to("copy failed", true));
  v.querySelector("[data-x=dl]").onclick = () => {
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([txt], {type:"text/markdown"}));
    a.download = "report-" + (p.slug || p.id) + ".md"; a.click();
  };
  v.querySelector("[data-x=l5]").onclick = () => act("l5", p);
}

let TOOLS = null;
async function renderMethods(v, p){
  if (!TOOLS) TOOLS = await get("/api/tools") || [];
  const langs = new Set((() => { try { return JSON.parse(p.type_all || "[]"); } catch { return []; } })());
  if (p.toolchain !== "web" && p.toolchain !== "unknown") langs.add(p.toolchain);
  if (p.toolchain === "web") langs.add("js");
  const ok = t => t.web ? p.toolchain === "web"
    : (!t.langs.length || t.langs.some(l => langs.has(l)));
  const rows = TOOLS.slice().sort((a,b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>${rows.length} methods · ${TOOLS.filter(t => t.fund).length} fund</h2>
    <p class="hint" style="margin-bottom:10px">applicable rows are live, the rest dimmed.</p>
    <table><thead><tr><th>id</th><th>method</th><th>class</th><th>sev</th><th>lvl</th>
    <th></th></tr></thead><tbody>` + rows.map(t => `<tr style="${ok(t) ? "" : "opacity:.35"}">
      <td class="mono">${esc(t.id)}</td>
      <td>${t.fund ? '<span class="chip cash">$</span> ' : ""}${esc(t.name)}</td>
      <td>${esc(t.cls)}</td><td class="sev sv-${esc(t.sev)}">${esc(t.sev)}</td>
      <td>L${t.lvl}</td>
      <td>${ok(t) ? `<button data-t="${esc(t.id)}">run</button>` : ""}</td></tr>`).join("") +
    `</tbody></table>`;
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-t]").forEach(b => b.onclick = async () => {
    try{ await post("/api/tool", {pid:p.id, id:b.dataset.t}); to(b.dataset.t + " queued"); poll(); }
    catch(e){ to(e.message, true); }
  });
}

  if (["INPUT","TEXTAREA","SELECT"].includes(e.target.tagName)) return;
  if (e.key === "/"){ e.preventDefault(); $("#q").focus(); return; }
  const p = cur();
  if (e.key === "a" && p) act("all", p);
  if (e.key === "s" && p) act("stop", p);
  if (e.key === "r" && p) act("restart", p);
  if (e.key === "j" || e.key === "k"){
    const ps = S ? S.projects : [];
    const i = ps.findIndex(x => x.id === SEL);
    const n = e.key === "j" ? Math.min(ps.length-1, i+1) : Math.max(0, i-1);
    if (ps[n]) select(ps[n].id);
  }
});

selftest.py must print selftest ok. dump.py must show an asset table, a severity table and a submission channel. Then run.bat.

What you will see change: the UI answers immediately because serve_forever() starts before the browser is launched; a hb: line lands every 20 s so "stuck" is a visible absence rather than a guess; discovery stands down whenever a job is queued or running; and the Health tab gives you uptime, queue depth, DB latency and network penalty so you can tell a busy engine from a dead one at a glance.

One honest caveat: your current build reports 162 methods, and the FUND table in what you pasted is truncated mid-F33. If selftest reports fewer than 165, the missing methods are in that truncated tail — send me the end of the FUND list and I will complete it rather than have you discover the gap at runtime.

add manual projects entering option in app, give me full updated codes files

Worked for 2m 57s · 1 step

Manual entry is the right call — private programs, PDF-only pages and invite-only briefs have no public source, so nothing should depend on a fetcher. I've added a first-class manual path that bypasses the details gate and the type resolver entirely, keeps every downstream rule (scope, tiers, PoC, submission channel) intact, and is fully editable after the fact.

Full files below. manual.py is new; bh.py, ui.html, platforms.py, selftest.py, reset.py, dump.py, run.bat and .env.example are complete replacements.

manual.py
python
"""Manual program entry: presets, parsing, validation, normalisation.

Manual projects skip the detail gate and the type resolver. Every other rule
(scope, tiers, poc, submission channel) still applies downstream.
"""
import re

KNOWN = {"solidity", "rust", "go", "python", "js", "php", "ruby", "java", "c", "web", "unknown"}
DRAFT_MIN = 2

PRESETS = {
    "evm": {
        "label": "EVM / Solidity",
        "toolchain": "solidity",
        "poc": True,
        "tiers": [("critical", 1000000), ("high", 50000), ("medium", 10000), ("low", 1000)],
        "template": ["summary", "affected", "impact", "likelihood", "poc", "fix"],
        "submit": ("email", ""),
    },
    "solana": {
        "label": "Solana / Rust",
        "toolchain": "rust",
        "poc": True,
        "tiers": [("critical", 500000), ("high", 25000), ("medium", 5000), ("low", 500)],
        "template": ["summary", "affected", "impact", "poc", "fix"],
        "submit": ("email", ""),
    },
    "web": {
        "label": "Web app / API",
        "toolchain": "web",
        "poc": False,
        "tiers": [("critical", 20000), ("high", 5000), ("medium", 1000), ("low", 200)],
        "template": ["summary", "steps", "impact", "fix"],
        "submit": ("form", ""),
    },
    "generic": {
        "label": "Generic source repo",
        "toolchain": "unknown",
        "poc": False,
        "tiers": [("critical", 10000), ("high", 2000), ("medium", 500), ("low", 100)],
        "template": ["summary", "affected", "impact", "fix"],
        "submit": ("email", ""),
    },
}

ADDR = re.compile(r"\b0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}\b")
SOL = re.compile(r"\b[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,44}\b")
REPO = re.compile(r"\b(?:https?://)?(?:www\.)?github\.com/([\w.-]+)/([\w.-]+)")
DOMAIN = re.compile(r"\b(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z]{2,24}\b", re.I)
MONEY = re.compile(r"\$\s?([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*([kKmM])?")
TIERLINE = re.compile(r"\b(critical|high|medium|low|informational|info)\b[^\n$]{0,60}?"
                      r"\$\s?([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*([kKmM])?", re.I)
EMAIL = re.compile(r"\b[\w.+-]+@[\w-]+\.[\w.]{2,}\b")
EXCL_HEAD = re.compile(r"out of scope|exclusion|not in scope|non-?vuln|non-?submission", re.I)

SCALE = {"k": 1_000, "m": 1_000_000}


def presets():
    return [{"id": k, "label": v["label"], "toolchain": v["toolchain"], "poc": v["poc"],
             "tiers": [{"tier": t, "max": m} for t, m in v["tiers"]],
             "template": v["template"], "submit": v["submit"][0]} for k, v in PRESETS.items()]


def _money(num, suf):
    try:
        v = float(num.replace(",", ""))
    except ValueError:
        return 0
    return int(v * SCALE.get((suf or "").lower(), 1))


def parse_block(text):
    """Best-effort draft from a pasted program page. Never raises."""
    text = text or ""
    d = {"name": "", "url": "", "toolchain": "unknown", "max": 0, "rank": 60,
         "scope": [], "tiers": [], "poc": False, "channel": "", "address": "",
         "exclusions": [], "template": [], "notes": ""}

    for line in text.splitlines()[:12]:
        s = line.strip()
        if s and not d["name"] and 2 <= len(s) <= 80 and not s.lower().startswith("http"):
            d["name"] = s
            break

    m = re.search(r"https?://[^\s)>\]]+", text)
    if m:
        d["url"] = m.group(0).rstrip(".,;")

    r = REPO.search(text)
    if r:
        d["scope"].append("https://github.com/%s/%s" % (r.group(1), r.group(2).rstrip(".")))
        if d["toolchain"] == "unknown":
            d["toolchain"] = "rust"

    for a in ADDR.findall(text):
        if a not in d["scope"]:
            d["scope"].append(a)

    if not d["scope"]:
        for s in SOL.findall(text):
            if len(s) >= 32 and not s.startswith("0x") and s not in d["scope"]:
                d["scope"].append(s)
                if d["toolchain"] == "unknown":
                    d["toolchain"] = "rust"

    low = text.lower()
    if re.search(r"\bsolidity|smart contract|\bevm\b|erc-?20", low) and d["toolchain"] == "unknown":
        d["toolchain"] = "solidity"
    if re.search(r"\banchor\b|\bsolana\b|\bpda\b", low):
        d["toolchain"] = "rust"

    if not d["scope"]:
        for h in DOMAIN.findall(text):
            h = h.lower()
            if h.endswith((".png", ".jpg", ".svg", ".js", ".css")):
                continue
            d["scope"].append(h)
            if d["toolchain"] == "unknown" and not re.match(r"^docs?\.", h):
                d["toolchain"] = "web"
            if len(d["scope"]) >= 6:
                break

    for m2 in TIERLINE.finditer(text):
        tier = m2.group(1).lower().replace("informational", "info")
        d["tiers"].append((tier, _money(m2.group(2), m2.group(3))))
    if d["tiers"]:
        d["max"] = max(v for _, v in d["tiers"])

    if re.search(r"\b(poc|proof of concept)\b[^\n]{0,40}\b(required|must|mandatory)\b", low):
        d["poc"] = True

    e = EMAIL.search(text)
    if e:
        d["channel"], d["address"] = "email", e.group(0)
    elif re.search(r"\bsubmit[^\n]{0,40}\bform\b", low):
        d["channel"] = "form"

    grab = False
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if EXCL_HEAD.search(line):
            grab = True
            continue
        if grab:
            s = line.strip(" -*\t")
            if not s:
                if d["exclusions"]:
                    grab = False
                continue
            if len(s) < 120:
                d["exclusions"].append(s)
            if len(d["exclusions"]) >= 25:
                break

    p = PRESETS.get(d["toolchain"] if d["toolchain"] in PRESETS else "generic")
    d["template"] = list(p["template"])
    return d


def _scope(raw):
    out = []
    for item in raw if isinstance(raw, list) else str(raw or "").splitlines():
        s = str(item).strip().strip(",")
        if not s:
            continue
        r = REPO.search(s)
        if r:
            s = "https://github.com/%s/%s" % (r.group(1), r.group(2).rstrip("."))
        if s not in out:
            out.append(s)
    return out[:300]


def _tiers(raw):
    out = []
    for row in raw or []:
        if isinstance(row, dict):
            tier, mx = row.get("tier"), row.get("max")
        else:
            tier, mx = (list(row) + [0])[:2]
        tier = str(tier or "").lower().replace("informational", "info")
        if tier not in ("critical", "high", "medium", "low", "info"):
            continue
        try:
            mx = max(0, min(10_000_000, int(float(mx or 0))))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            mx = 0
        if tier not in [t for t, _ in out]:
            out.append((tier, mx))
    return out


def validate(d, draft=False):
    """Return (clean, errors). Errors are user-facing strings."""
    errs = []
    name = str(d.get("name") or "").strip()[:80]
    if len(name) < DRAFT_MIN:
        errs.append("name needs at least %d characters" % DRAFT_MIN)

    url = str(d.get("url") or "").strip()[:400]
    if url and not re.match(r"^https?://", url) and not REPO.search(url) \
            and not ADDR.fullmatch(url) and not SOL.fullmatch(url):
        errs.append("url must be http(s), a github repo, or an address")

    toolchain = str(d.get("toolchain") or "unknown").lower()
    if toolchain not in KNOWN:
        toolchain = "unknown"

    try:
        mx = max(0, min(10_000_000, int(float(d.get("max") or 0))))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        mx = 0
        errs.append("max bounty must be a number")

    try:
        rank = max(1, min(100, int(float(d.get("rank") or 60))))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        rank = 60

    scope = _scope(d.get("scope"))
    tiers = _tiers(d.get("tiers"))
    if not draft and not scope:
        errs.append("at least one scope asset is required")

    poc = d.get("poc")
    if isinstance(poc, str):
        poc = poc.strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")
    poc = bool(poc) or toolchain in ("solidity", "rust")

    channel = str(d.get("channel") or "").strip().lower()
    if channel not in ("email", "form", "platform", "other", ""):
        channel = "other"
    address = str(d.get("address") or "").strip()[:300]
    if channel == "email" and address and not EMAIL.search(address):
        errs.append("submission address is not an email")

    exclusions = [str(s).strip()[:200] for s in (d.get("exclusions") or []) if str(s).strip()][:60]
    template = [str(s).strip()[:40] for s in (d.get("template") or []) if str(s).strip()][:12]

    clean = {"name": name, "url": url, "toolchain": toolchain, "max": mx, "rank": rank,
             "scope": scope, "tiers": tiers, "poc": poc, "channel": channel,
             "address": address, "exclusions": exclusions, "template": template,
             "notes": str(d.get("notes") or "").strip()[:4000], "draft": bool(draft)}
    return clean, errs


def slugify(name, pid=0):
    s = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", (name or "").lower()).strip("-")[:48]
    return "%s-%d" % (s or "manual", pid)


def conditions_from(c):
    """Map a validated draft onto contract.save_conditions kwargs."""
    return {
        "scope_model": "manual",
        "targets": [{"address": a, "chain": "manual", "kind": "manual"} for a in c["scope"]],
        "severity": [{"tier": t, "band": ("<=$%d" % m) if m else "", "definition": ""}
                     for t, m in c["tiers"]],
        "poc": {"required": c["poc"], "framework": "", "source": "manual"},
        "exclusions": {"surfaces": c["exclusions"], "non_vulns": [], "non_submissions": []},
        "submit": {"channel": c["channel"], "address": c["address"], "subject_fmt": ""},
        "template": c["template"],
        "one_page": False,
        "constraints": {},
    }


def bulk(text):
    """One line per program: name | url | type | max | asset,asset,asset"""
    made, bad = [], []
    for i, line in enumerate((text or "").splitlines(), 1):
        line = line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            continue
        parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split("|")]
        while len(parts) < 5:
            parts.append("")
        d = {"name": parts[0], "url": parts[1], "toolchain": parts[2] or "unknown",
             "max": parts[3], "scope": [s for s in re.split(r"[,\s]+", parts[4]) if s]}
        clean, errs = validate(d)
        if errs:
            bad.append((i, line[:60], "; ".join(errs)))
        else:
            made.append(clean)
    return made, bad
bh.py
