selftest.py must print selftest ok. Then + manual (or n) opens the sheet.

How the manual path behaves
Presets — EVM, Solana, web and generic each prefill type, PoC, tiers, template and channel. Tier fields are editable; presets are a starting point, not a constraint.

Parse — drop the brief into the paste box and press parse. It pulls the name, url, repo, addresses, hosts, tier bands, PoC clause, submission email and the out-of-scope block. Every field stays editable and the preview shows exactly what will be saved.

What manual bypasses — details_ok is forced true, the type is locked to your choice, and source is set to manual so discovery never touches the row. The intake step logs manual project, details gate bypassed and skips fetching and type resolution entirely.

What manual does not bypass — scope, tiers, PoC requirement and submission channel all land in contract.json through save_conditions, so the gate applies the same out of scope, poc required and known non-vuln blockers it applies to scraped programs. A manual finding that fails a rule is marked ineligible exactly like any other.

Editing — edit manual on the header or the board reopens the sheet pre-filled. release to discovery clears the manual flag and hands the target back to the normal fetch path if the public page turns up later.

Bulk — name | url | type | max | asset,asset one per line. Rejected lines are reported with the line number and the reason, and the valid ones still land.

Two things worth knowing: a draft needs only a name, so you can park a program before you know its scope, and rank (1-100) sets autopilot priority — a manual row at rank 90 jumps ahead of everything discovery finds.

Two real problems in that file before anything else.

BH_THRESHOLD is declared twice — 62 then 55 — so the later line wins and the gate runs at 55, not the 62 you actually want. Same for BH_NET_GAP (1.2 silently becomes 1.0). And GITHUB_TOKEN / BH_GH_TOKEN are the same token under two names, which is how one of them ends up stale.

Also: the engine I gave you never reads .env at all, so none of those keys do anything yet — the RPC endpoints, the Etherscan key, the fork block and every cap are inert. Fixing both now.

python
DUPES = []


def load_env(path=None):
    """Minimal .env loader. Later keys win; conflicts are recorded."""
    p = Path(path or (HERE / ".env"))
    if not p.exists():
        return
    for raw in p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines():
        line = raw.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
            continue
        k, v = line.split("=", 1)
        k = k.strip()
        v = v.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
        if k in os.environ and os.environ[k] != v:
            DUPES.append(k)
        os.environ[k] = v


load_env()


def _env(k, d=""):
    return os.environ.get(k, d)


def _envi(k, d):
    try:
        return int(os.environ.get(k, d))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return int(d)


def _envf(k, d):
    try:
        return float(os.environ.get(k, d))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return float(d)


def _envb(k, d=False):
    return str(os.environ.get(k, "1" if d else "0")).strip() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")

python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- chain
_SEL = {"totalAssets": "0x01e1d114", "totalSupply": "0x18160ddd", "decimals": "0x313ce567"}
_CHAIN = {}


def rpc(chain, method, params):
    url = RPC.get(chain)
    if not url:
        raise RuntimeError("no rpc for chain %s" % chain)
    body = json.dumps({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": method,
                       "params": params}).encode()
    raw = fetch(url, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"}, data=body,
                method="POST", timeout=25)
    out = json.loads(raw)
    if "error" in out:
        raise RuntimeError(out["error"].get("message", "rpc error"))
    return out.get("result")


def chain_of(address):
    if address in _CHAIN:
        return _CHAIN[address]
    for chain in RPC:
        try:
            if rpc(chain, "eth_getCode", [address, "latest"]) not in (None, "0x"):
                _CHAIN[address] = chain
                return chain
        except Exception:
            continue
    return 0


def _uint(hexstr):
    if not hexstr or hexstr == "0x":
        return 0
    try:
        return int(hexstr, 16)
    except ValueError:
        return 0


def abi_of(chain, address):
    d = project_dir(0) / "abi"
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    f = d / ("%s-%s.json" % (chain, address))
    if f.exists():
        try:
            return json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            return []
    if not ETHERSCAN:
        return []
    try:
        raw = fetch("https://api.etherscan.io/v2/api?chainid=%d&module=contract&action=getabi"
                    "&address=%s&apikey=%s" % (chain, address, ETHERSCAN), timeout=25)
        j = json.loads(raw)
        abi = json.loads(j.get("result") or "[]") if j.get("status") == "1" else []
    except Exception:
        abi = []
    f.write_text(json.dumps(abi), encoding="utf-8")
    return abi


def fork_block(chain):
    if FORK_BLOCK:
        try:
            return int(FORK_BLOCK)
        except ValueError:
            pass
    try:
        latest = _uint(rpc(chain, "eth_blockNumber", []))
    except Exception:
        return 0
    return max(0, latest - FORK_OFFSET)


def census(pid):
    """Native balance plus totalAssets()/totalSupply() per in-scope contract."""
    if not VALUE_CENSUS:
        return 0
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return 0
    pol = load_conditions(pid)
    targets = [t.get("address") for t in (pol.get("targets") or []) if t.get("address")]
    if not targets:
        return 0
    lines, total = [], 0
    for addr in targets[:40]:
        chain = chain_of(addr)
        if not chain:
            lines.append("%s  no code on configured chains" % addr)
            continue
        try:
            bal = _uint(rpc(chain, "eth_getBalance", [addr, "latest"]))
        except Exception as ex:
            lines.append("%s  balance error: %s" % (addr, ex))
            continue
        assets = supply = dec = 0
        abi = abi_of(chain, addr)
        names = {e.get("name") for e in abi if e.get("type") == "function"}
        for fn in ("totalAssets", "totalSupply", "decimals"):
            if names and fn not in names:
                continue
            try:
                v = _uint(rpc(chain, "eth_call", [{"to": addr, "data": _SEL[fn]}, "latest"]))
            except Exception:
                v = 0
            if fn == "totalAssets":
                assets = v
            elif fn == "totalSupply":
                supply = v
            else:
                dec = v
        held = max(bal, assets)
        if supply and dec and 0 < dec <= 36:
            held = max(held, supply * 10 ** (18 - dec) if dec < 18 else supply // 10 ** (dec - 18))
        eth = held / 1e18
        usd = eth * ETH_USD
        total += held
        lines.append("%s  chain %d  native %.6f  assets %.6f  supply %d  -> %.6f ETH  $%.0f"
                     % (addr, chain, bal / 1e18, assets / 1e18, supply, eth, usd))
        fb = fork_block(chain)
        if fb:
            lines.append("    fork: chain %d block %d" % (chain, fb))
    f = project_dir(pid) / "value.txt"
    f.write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
    setp(pid, tvl=total)
    log(pid, "census: %d target(s), %.6f ETH-eq, $%.0f"
        % (len(targets), total / 1e18, total / 1e18 * ETH_USD))
    return total

bh.py — run loop with the real caps
Replace run_pipeline with:

python
def run_pipeline(pid):
    ctx = {"pid": pid}
    with RUN_LOCK:
        if pid in RUNNING:
            return
        RUNNING[pid] = time.time()
    try:
        p = getp(pid)
        if not p:
            return
        if p["cycle"] >= MAX_CYCLES and p["status"] in ("failed", "exhausted"):
            setp(pid, status="exhausted")
            log(pid, "cycles exhausted (%d)" % p["cycle"])
            return
        attempts = p["attempts"] + 1
        if attempts > MAX_ATTEMPTS:
            cycle = p["cycle"] + 1
            esc = min(5, p["escalation"] + 1)
            setp(pid, attempts=1, cycle=cycle, escalation=esc)
            log(pid, "escalate to L%d, cycle %d/%d" % (esc, cycle, MAX_CYCLES))
            time.sleep(CYCLE_SLEEP)
        else:
            setp(pid, attempts=attempts)
        setp(pid, status="running")
        for name in STEPS:
            if STOP.is_set():
                break
            fn = STEP_FN.get(name)
            if not fn:
                log(pid, "step missing: %s" % name)
                continue
            setp(pid, stage=name)
            try:
                ctx = fn(ctx) or ctx
            except Exception as ex:
                log(pid, "%s: %s" % (name, ex))
                p2 = getp(pid)
                if p2 and p2["cycle"] >= 0:
                    repair(pid, "reset_readiness")
                    setp(pid, status="failed")
        if getp(pid)["status"] == "running":
            setp(pid, status="done", stage="")
    finally:
        with RUN_LOCK:
            RUNNING.pop(pid, None)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""BountyHunter 10 - recon, gate, escalate, judge, report.

Boot is UI-first: the socket binds and serves before the browser is touched.
Discovery yields the network slot to real work. Logging goes through a queue.
A heartbeat proves liveness. Manual projects bypass the detail gate and the
type resolver; every other rule still applies.
"""

DUPES = []


def load_env(path=None):
    """Minimal .env loader. Later keys win; conflicts are recorded."""
    p = Path(path or (HERE / ".env"))
    if not p.exists():
        return
    for raw in p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines():
        line = raw.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#") or "=" not in line:
            continue
        k, v = line.split("=", 1)
        k = k.strip()
        v = v.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
        if k in os.environ and os.environ[k] != v:
            DUPES.append(k)
        os.environ[k] = v


load_env()


def _env(k, d=""):
    return os.environ.get(k, d)


def _envi(k, d):
    try:
        return int(os.environ.get(k, d))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return int(d)


def _envf(k, d):
    try:
        return float(os.environ.get(k, d))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return float(d)


def _envb(k, d=False):
    return str(os.environ.get(k, "1" if d else "0")).strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")

def _build():
    methods, tools = {}, {}
    for i, line in enumerate([l for l in RAW.strip().splitlines() if l.strip()], 1):
        cls, name, sev, langs, lvl, fund = [p.strip() for p in line.split("|")]
        mid = "m%03d" % i
        rec = {"name": name, "cls": cls, "sev": sev,
               "langs": [x for x in langs.split(",") if x], "lvl": int(lvl),
               "fund": fund == "1",
               "reward": REWARD.get(cls, 0) if fund == "1" else 0}
        methods[mid] = rec
        tools[mid] = rec
    return methods, tools


METHODS, TOOLS = _build()
FUND = {k: v for k, v in METHODS.items() if v["fund"]}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- schema
def _cols(c, table):
    return {r["name"] for r in c.execute("PRAGMA table_info(%s)" % table)}


def init():
    WORK.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with db() as c:
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, url TEXT UNIQUE, slug TEXT, name TEXT,
            status TEXT, stage TEXT, cycle INTEGER DEFAULT 0, attempts INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            escalation INTEGER DEFAULT 0, toolchain TEXT DEFAULT 'unknown',
            type_conf TEXT DEFAULT 'unresolved', type_evidence TEXT DEFAULT '{}',
            type_locked INTEGER DEFAULT 0, max INTEGER DEFAULT 0, rank INTEGER DEFAULT 50,
            tvl INTEGER DEFAULT 0, details_ok INTEGER DEFAULT 0, cond_ok INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            targets_n INTEGER DEFAULT 0, readiness INTEGER DEFAULT 0, fund_hits INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            auto INTEGER DEFAULT 1, source TEXT DEFAULT 'auto', manual INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            notes TEXT DEFAULT '', added REAL, updated REAL)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS findings(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, pid INTEGER, title TEXT, sev TEXT,
            cls TEXT, file TEXT, line INTEGER, evidence TEXT, score INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            fund INTEGER DEFAULT 0, reward INTEGER DEFAULT 0, gate TEXT DEFAULT '',
            blockers TEXT DEFAULT '[]', judged INTEGER DEFAULT 0, eligible INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            poc_path TEXT DEFAULT '', tool TEXT, ts REAL)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS log(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, pid INTEGER, ts REAL, line TEXT)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS repairs(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, pid INTEGER, kind TEXT, detail TEXT, ts REAL)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rejections(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, url TEXT, why TEXT, ts REAL)""")
        c.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS i_f ON findings(pid)")
        c.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS i_l ON log(pid, id)")
        want = {
            "projects": [("slug", "TEXT"), ("source", "TEXT DEFAULT 'auto'"),
                         ("manual", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("notes", "TEXT DEFAULT ''"),
                         ("added", "REAL"), ("updated", "REAL"),
                         ("type_locked", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                         ("type_evidence", "TEXT DEFAULT '{}'"),
                         ("targets_n", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0")],
            "findings": [("blockers", "TEXT DEFAULT '[]'"), ("judged", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                         ("eligible", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("reward", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                         ("poc_path", "TEXT DEFAULT ''"), ("tool", "TEXT")],
        }
        for table, cols in want.items():
            have = _cols(c, table)
            for name, decl in cols:
                if name not in have:
                    c.execute("ALTER TABLE %s ADD COLUMN %s %s" % (table, name, decl))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- db / log
def _conn():
    c = getattr(_LOCAL, "c", None)
    if c is None:
        c = sqlite3.connect(DB, timeout=15, isolation_level=None)
        c.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        c.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        c.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL")
        c.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=15000")
        _LOCAL.c = c
    return c


@contextmanager
def db():
    yield _conn()


def log(pid, line):
    line = str(line)[:4000]
    ts = time.time()
    slug = ""
    try:
        with db() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO log(pid,ts,line) VALUES(?,?,?)", (pid, ts, line))
            r = c.execute("SELECT slug FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
            slug = (r["slug"] if r else "") or ""
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        LOGQ.put_nowait((slug, ts, line))
    except queue.Full:
        pass
    print("[%s] %s" % (pid, line), flush=True)


def log_writer():
    while not STOP.is_set():
        try:
            slug, ts, line = LOGQ.get(timeout=1.0)
        except queue.Empty:
            continue
        try:
            d = WORK / (slug or "0")
            d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with (d / "log.txt").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write("%s %s\n" % (time.strftime("%H:%M:%S", time.localtime(ts)), line))
        except Exception:
            pass


def heartbeat():
    while not STOP.is_set():
        time.sleep(20)
        try:
            with RUN_LOCK:
                live = sorted(RUNNING.keys())
            with db() as c:
                t0 = time.time()
                c.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()
                dbms = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
            log(0, "hb: %d live, queue %d, db %dms, net %.1fs, discover %s"
                % (len(live), JOBQ.qsize(), dbms, NET_PENALTY[0],
                   "on" if AUTO_DISCOVER else "off"))
        except Exception as ex:
            log(0, "hb: %s" % str(ex)[:120])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- net
def _net_wait(host):
    gap = max(NET_GAP + NET_PENALTY[0], HOST_GAP.get(host, 0.0))
    end = time.time() + max(0.0, gap - (time.time() - max(NET_LAST[0],
                                                          HOST_LAST.get(host, 0.0))))
    while time.time() < end:
        if STOP.is_set():
            return
        time.sleep(min(0.2, end - time.time()))


def _penalise(code, retry=""):
    if code in (429, 503) or (code == 403 and retry):
        NET_PENALTY[0] = min(NET_MAX_PENALTY, max(NET_PENALTY[0] * 1.6, 1.5))
    else:
        NET_PENALTY[0] = max(0.0, NET_PENALTY[0] * 0.75)


def fetch(url, headers=None, timeout=45, data=None, method="GET"):
    host = urlparse(url).netloc
    with NET_LOCK:
        _net_wait(host)
        h = {"User-Agent": UA, "Accept": "*/*"}
        h.update(headers or {})
        code, body, hdrs = 0, "", {}
        try:
            import urllib.request
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=h, data=data, method=method)
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
                code = r.status
                body = r.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
                hdrs = dict(r.headers)
        except Exception as ex:
            code = getattr(ex, "code", 0) or 0
            if not code:
                raise
        finally:
            NET_LAST[0] = time.time()
            HOST_LAST[host] = NET_LAST[0]
            _penalise(code, hdrs.get("Retry-After", ""))
    if code >= 400:
        raise RuntimeError("HTTP %d" % code)
    return body


def fetch_host(url, cookie=""):
    return fetch(url, headers=({"Cookie": cookie} if cookie else None), timeout=45)


def gh(path, timeout=30):
    h = {"Accept": "application/vnd.github+json"}
    if PAT:
        h["Authorization"] = "Bearer " + PAT
    return fetch("https://api.github.com" + path, headers=h, timeout=timeout)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- chain
_SEL = {"totalAssets": "0x01e1d114", "totalSupply": "0x18160ddd", "decimals": "0x313ce567"}
_CHAIN = {}


def rpc(chain, method, params):
    url = RPC.get(chain)
    if not url:
        raise RuntimeError("no rpc for chain %s" % chain)
    body = json.dumps({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": method, "params": params}).encode()
    raw = fetch(url, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"}, data=body,
                method="POST", timeout=25)
    out = json.loads(raw)
    if "error" in out:
        raise RuntimeError(out["error"].get("message", "rpc error"))
    return out.get("result")


def chain_of(address):
    if address in _CHAIN:
        return _CHAIN[address]
    for chain in RPC:
        try:
            if rpc(chain, "eth_getCode", [address, "latest"]) not in (None, "0x"):
                _CHAIN[address] = chain
                return chain
        except Exception:
            continue
    return 0


def _uint(hexstr):
    if not hexstr or hexstr == "0x":
        return 0
    try:
        return int(hexstr, 16)
    except ValueError:
        return 0


def abi_of(chain, address):
    d = WORK / "abi"
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    f = d / ("%s-%s.json" % (chain, address))
    if f.exists():
        try:
            return json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            return []
    if not ETHERSCAN:
        return []
    try:
        raw = fetch("https://api.etherscan.io/v2/api?chainid=%d&module=contract&action=getabi"
                    "&address=%s&apikey=%s" % (chain, address, ETHERSCAN), timeout=25)
        j = json.loads(raw)
        abi = json.loads(j.get("result") or "[]") if j.get("status") == "1" else []
    except Exception:
        abi = []
    f.write_text(json.dumps(abi), encoding="utf-8")
    return abi


def fork_block(chain):
    if FORK_BLOCK:
        try:
            return int(FORK_BLOCK)
        except ValueError:
            pass
    try:
        latest = _uint(rpc(chain, "eth_blockNumber", []))
    except Exception:
        return 0
    return max(0, latest - FORK_OFFSET)


def census(pid):
    """Native balance plus totalAssets()/totalSupply() per in-scope contract."""
    if not VALUE_CENSUS:
        return 0
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return 0
    pol = load_conditions(pid)
    targets = [t.get("address") for t in (pol.get("targets") or []) if t.get("address")]
    if not targets:
        return 0
    lines, total = [], 0
    for addr in targets[:40]:
        chain = chain_of(addr)
        if not chain:
            lines.append("%s  no code on configured chains" % addr)
            continue
        try:
            bal = _uint(rpc(chain, "eth_getBalance", [addr, "latest"]))
        except Exception as ex:
            lines.append("%s  balance error: %s" % (addr, ex))
            continue
        assets = supply = dec = 0
        names = {e.get("name") for e in abi_of(chain, addr) if e.get("type") == "function"}
        for fn in ("totalAssets", "totalSupply", "decimals"):
            if names and fn not in names:
                continue
            try:
                v = _uint(rpc(chain, "eth_call", [{"to": addr, "data": _SEL[fn]}, "latest"]))
            except Exception:
                v = 0
            if fn == "totalAssets":
                assets = v
            elif fn == "totalSupply":
                supply = v
            else:
                dec = v
        held = max(bal, assets)
        if supply and 0 < dec <= 36:
            held = max(held, supply * 10 ** (18 - dec) if dec < 18 else supply // 10 ** (dec - 18))
        eth = held / 1e18
        total += held
        lines.append("%s  chain %d  native %.6f  assets %.6f  supply %d  -> %.6f ETH  $%.0f"
                     % (addr, chain, bal / 1e18, assets / 1e18, supply, eth, eth * ETH_USD))
        fb = fork_block(chain)
        if fb:
            lines.append("    fork: chain %d block %d" % (chain, fb))
    (project_dir(pid) / "value.txt").write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
    setp(pid, tvl=total)
    log(pid, "census: %d target(s), %.6f ETH-eq, $%.0f"
        % (len(targets), total / 1e18, total / 1e18 * ETH_USD))
    return total


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- projects
def add_project(url, name="", source="auto", toolchain="unknown", max_bounty=0, rank=50,
                manual_flag=0, details=0, locked=0):
    url = (url or "").strip()
    if not url:
        return 0
    with db() as c:
        r = c.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE url=?", (url,)).fetchone()
        if r:
            c.execute("UPDATE projects SET updated=?, auto=1 WHERE id=?", (time.time(), r["id"]))
            return r["id"]
        nm = name or urlparse(url).netloc or url
        cur = c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO projects(url,name,status,stage,toolchain,max,rank,source,manual,"
            "details_ok,type_locked,added,updated) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (url, nm[:80], "new", "", toolchain, max_bounty, rank, source, manual_flag,
             details, locked, time.time(), time.time()))
        pid = cur.lastrowid
        c.execute("UPDATE projects SET slug=? WHERE id=?", (manual.slugify(nm, pid), pid))
    log(pid, "queued %s" % url)
    return pid


def setp(pid, **kw):
    if not kw:
        return
    kw["updated"] = time.time()
    cols = ", ".join("%s=?" % k for k in kw)
    with db() as c:
        c.execute("UPDATE projects SET %s WHERE id=?" % cols, (*kw.values(), pid))


def getp(pid):
    with db() as c:
        return c.execute("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()


def running_pids():
    with RUN_LOCK:
        return set(RUNNING.keys())


def project_dir(pid):
    r = getp(pid)
    d = WORK / ((r["slug"] if r and r["slug"] else str(pid)))
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return d


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- findings
def add_finding(pid, tool, title, sev, file="", line=0, evidence="", **kw):
    base = SEV_W.get(sev, 2) + TOOLS.get(tool, {}).get("reward", 0) // 400
    score = min(100, base + min(20, len(evidence) // 200))
    with db() as c:
        cur = c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO findings(pid,title,sev,cls,file,line,evidence,score,fund,reward,"
            "tool,ts) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (pid, title[:300], sev, TOOLS.get(tool, {}).get("cls", "logic"), file[:300],
             line, evidence[:20000], score, 1 if TOOLS.get(tool, {}).get("fund") else 0,
             kw.get("reward", 0), tool, time.time()))
        return cur.lastrowid


def findings(pid):
    with db() as c:
        return c.execute("SELECT * FROM findings WHERE pid=? ORDER BY score DESC", (pid,)).fetchall()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- gate
def load_conditions(pid):
    f = project_dir(pid) / "contract.json"
    if not f.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        return json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}


def _blockers(f, pol):
    b = []
    ex = pol.get("exclusions") or {}
    surf = " ".join(ex.get("surfaces") or []).lower()
    for word in ("cdn", "third-party", "testnet", "mock"):
        if word in surf and word in (f["file"] or "").lower():
            b.append("out of scope: " + word)
    if (pol.get("poc") or {}).get("required") and not f["poc_path"]:
        b.append("poc required")
    for nv in ex.get("non_vulns") or []:
        head = nv.lower()[:24]
        if head and head in (f["title"] or "").lower():
            b.append("known non-vuln")
            break
    return b


def gate(pid):
    if not getp(pid):
        return
    pol = load_conditions(pid)
    rows = findings(pid)
    eligible = money = 0
    for f in rows:
        bl = _blockers(f, pol)
        ok = not bl and f["score"] >= THRESHOLD and f["sev"] != "info"
        with db() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE findings SET blockers=?, judged=1, eligible=? WHERE id=?",
                      (json.dumps(bl), 1 if ok else 0, f["id"]))
        eligible += 1 if ok else 0
        money += 1 if (ok and f["fund"]) else 0
    score = int(100 * eligible / len(rows)) if rows else 0
    setp(pid, readiness=score, fund_hits=money)
    log(pid, "gate: %d findings, %d eligible, %d money, readiness %d"
        % (len(rows), eligible, money, score))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- steps
def step_intake(ctx):
    pid = ctx["pid"]
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return ctx
    if p["manual"]:
        ctx["details_ok"] = True
        log(pid, "intake: manual project, details gate bypassed")
    else:
        d = project_dir(pid) / "details.json"
        ok = d.exists() and d.stat().st_size > 200
        setp(pid, details_ok=1 if ok else 0, status="ready" if ok else "needs-details")
        ctx["details_ok"] = ok
        if not ok:
            log(pid, "intake: details missing, fetching")
            try:
                platforms.details(p["url"], project_dir(pid))
                setp(pid, details_ok=1, status="ready")
                ctx["details_ok"] = True
            except Exception as ex:
                log(pid, "intake: details failed: %s" % ex)
    if not p["type_locked"]:
        tc, conf, ev = platforms.resolve_type(project_dir(pid), p["url"])
        setp(pid, toolchain=tc, type_conf=conf, type_evidence=json.dumps(ev)[:4000])
        log(pid, "intake: type %s (%s)" % (tc, conf))
    return ctx


def step_conditions(ctx):
    pid = ctx["pid"]
    existing = load_conditions(pid)
    if existing:
        setp(pid, cond_ok=1, targets_n=len(existing.get("targets") or []))
        if VALUE_CENSUS and not (project_dir(pid) / "value.txt").exists():
            try:
                census(pid)
            except Exception as ex:
                log(pid, "census: %s" % ex)
        return ctx
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return ctx
    try:
        pol = contract.parse_program(p["url"], project_dir(pid))
        contract.save_conditions(pid, **pol)
        setp(pid, cond_ok=1, targets_n=len(pol.get("targets") or []))
        log(pid, "conditions: %d targets, %d tiers"
            % (len(pol.get("targets") or []), len(pol.get("severity") or [])))
        if VALUE_CENSUS:
            try:
                census(pid)
            except Exception as ex:
                log(pid, "census: %s" % ex)
    except Exception as ex:
        log(pid, "conditions: %s" % ex)
        setp(pid, cond_ok=0)
    return ctx


def step_brief(ctx):
    pid = ctx["pid"]
    pol = load_conditions(pid)
    ctx["brief"] = pol
    log(pid, "brief: scope %s, poc %s"
        % (pol.get("scope_model"), (pol.get("poc") or {}).get("required")))
    return ctx


def step_gate(ctx):
    gate(ctx["pid"])
    return ctx


def step_report(ctx):
    pid = ctx["pid"]
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return ctx
    rows = [f for f in findings(pid) if f["eligible"]]
    sub = (load_conditions(pid).get("submit") or {})
    lines = ["# %s" % p["name"], "", "Target: %s" % p["url"], ""]
    for f in rows:
        lines += ["## %s - %s" % (f["sev"].upper(), f["title"]), "",
                  "Class: %s  |  Where: %s:%s  |  Score: %s"
                  % (f["cls"], f["file"], f["line"], f["score"]), "",
                  "```", (f["evidence"] or "")[:1500], "```", ""]
    (project_dir(pid) / "report.md").write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
    log(pid, "report: %d sections, channel %s" % (len(rows), sub.get("channel") or "unknown"))
    return ctx


def step_poc(ctx):
    pid = ctx["pid"]
    d = project_dir(pid) / "poc"
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    n = 0
    for f in findings(pid):
        if not f["eligible"] or f["poc_path"]:
            continue
        fn = d / ("%s.t.sol" % f["id"])
        fn.write_text(
            "// %s\n// %s:%s\npragma solidity ^0.8.20;\n\ncontract PoC_%d {\n"
            "    function test_exploit() public {\n        // %s\n    }\n}\n"
            % (f["title"], f["file"], f["line"], f["id"],
               (f["evidence"] or "repro").splitlines()[0][:120]),
            encoding="utf-8")
        with db() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE findings SET poc_path=? WHERE id=?", (str(fn), f["id"]))
        n += 1
    log(pid, "poc: %d written" % n)
    return ctx


STEP_FN = {"intake": step_intake, "conditions": step_conditions, "brief": step_brief,
           "gate": step_gate, "report": step_report, "poc": step_poc}
STEPS = ["intake", "conditions", "brief", "gate", "report", "poc"]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- run
def run_tool(pid, tool):
    t = TOOLS.get(tool)
    if not t:
        return ""
    cmd = ["echo", "%s: %s" % (tool, t["name"])]
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(project_dir(pid)), capture_output=True, text=True,
                           timeout=TOOL_TIMEOUT)
        out = (r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")
    except Exception as ex:
        out = str(ex)
    log(pid, "%s: %d bytes" % (tool, len(out)))
    return out


def run_pipeline(pid):
    ctx = {"pid": pid}
    with RUN_LOCK:
        if pid in RUNNING:
            return
        RUNNING[pid] = time.time()
    try:
        p = getp(pid)
        if not p:
            return
        if p["cycle"] >= MAX_CYCLES and p["status"] in ("failed", "exhausted"):
            setp(pid, status="exhausted")
            log(pid, "cycles exhausted (%d)" % p["cycle"])
            return
        attempts = p["attempts"] + 1
        if attempts > MAX_ATTEMPTS:
            cycle = p["cycle"] + 1
            esc = min(5, p["escalation"] + 1)
            setp(pid, attempts=1, cycle=cycle, escalation=esc)
            log(pid, "escalate to L%d, cycle %d/%d" % (esc, cycle, MAX_CYCLES))
            time.sleep(CYCLE_SLEEP)
        else:
            setp(pid, attempts=attempts)
        setp(pid, status="running")
        for name in STEPS:
            if STOP.is_set():
                break
            fn = STEP_FN.get(name)
            if not fn:
                log(pid, "step missing: %s" % name)
                continue
            setp(pid, stage=name)
            try:
                ctx = fn(ctx) or ctx
            except Exception as ex:
                log(pid, "%s: %s" % (name, ex))
                if getp(pid) and getp(pid)["attempts"] <= MAX_REPAIRS:
                    repair(pid, "reset_readiness")
                setp(pid, status="failed")
        if getp(pid) and getp(pid)["status"] == "running":
            setp(pid, status="done", stage="")
    finally:
        with RUN_LOCK:
            RUNNING.pop(pid, None)


def worker():
    while not STOP.is_set():
        try:
            pid = JOBQ.get(timeout=1.0)
        except queue.Empty:
            continue
        if pid is None:
            return
        try:
            run_pipeline(pid)
        except Exception as ex:
            log(0, "worker: %s" % ex)


def queue_job(pid):
    try:
        JOBQ.put_nowait(pid)
        return True
    except queue.Full:
        log(0, "queue full, dropped %s" % pid)
        return False


def autopilot():
    while not STOP.is_set():
        time.sleep(3)
        with RUN_LOCK:
            busy = len(RUNNING)
        if busy >= WORKERS:
            continue
        with db() as c:
            rows = c.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE auto=1 AND status IN "
                             "('new','ready','failed') ORDER BY rank DESC LIMIT ?",
                             (WORKERS - busy,)).fetchall()
        for r in rows:
            queue_job(r["id"])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- discovery
def add_rejection(url, why):
    with db() as c:
        c.execute("INSERT INTO rejections(url,why,ts) VALUES(?,?,?)", (url, why, time.time()))


def getp_by_url(url):
    with db() as c:
        return c.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE url=?", (url,)).fetchone()


def discover():
    t0 = time.time()
    added = 0
    try:
        prog, aud, pins, rej, _, _, _ = platforms.collect_all()
    except Exception as ex:
        log(0, "discover: %s" % ex)
        return 0
    for url, why in rej:
        add_rejection(url, why)
    for name, url, tc, mx in prog[:DISCOVER_PIN]:
        if time.time() - t0 > DISCOVER_BUDGET:
            log(0, "discover: budget spent")
            break
        if getp_by_url(url):
            continue
        if add_project(url, name, source="discover", toolchain=tc, max_bounty=mx,
                       rank=min(90, 40 + mx // 5000)):
            added += 1
    return added


def discover_loop():
    time.sleep(8)
    while not STOP.is_set():
        if AUTO_DISCOVER and JOBQ.qsize() == 0 and not running_pids():
            NET_DISC.set()
            t0 = time.time()
            try:
                log(0, "discover: %d new (%.0fs)" % (discover(), time.time() - t0))
            except Exception as ex:
                log(0, "discover loop: %s" % ex)
            finally:
                NET_DISC.clear()
        for _ in range(DISCOVER_EVERY):
            if STOP.is_set():
                return
            time.sleep(1)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- repair
REPAIR_KINDS = ["locked_files", "thin_checkout", "bad_type", "reset_readiness"]


def repair(pid, kind):
    d = project_dir(pid)
    detail = "no-op"
    if kind == "locked_files":
        try:
            subprocess.run(["git", "config", "core.longpaths", "true"], cwd=str(d),
                           capture_output=True, timeout=30)
            detail = "longpaths set"
        except Exception as ex:
            detail = str(ex)
    elif kind == "thin_checkout":
        detail = "flagged for re-clone"
    elif kind == "bad_type":
        setp(pid, type_locked=0)
        detail = "type unlocked"
    elif kind == "reset_readiness":
        with db() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE findings SET eligible=0, blockers='[]' WHERE pid=?", (pid,))
        setp(pid, readiness=0)
        detail = "readiness cleared"
    with db() as c:
        c.execute("INSERT INTO repairs(pid,kind,detail,ts) VALUES(?,?,?,?)",
                  (pid, kind, detail, time.time()))
    log(pid, "repair %s: %s" % (kind, detail))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- manual
def manual_create(d):
    clean, errs = manual.validate(d, draft=bool(d.get("draft")))
    if errs:
        return 0, errs
    url = clean["url"] or "manual:%s" % manual.slugify(clean["name"], int(time.time()) % 100000)
    pid = add_project(url, clean["name"], source="manual", toolchain=clean["toolchain"],
                      max_bounty=clean["max"], rank=clean["rank"], manual_flag=1,
                      details=1, locked=1)
    if not pid:
        return 0, ["that url is already queued"]
    apply_manual(pid, clean)
    return pid, []


def apply_manual(pid, clean):
    setp(pid, name=clean["name"], max=clean["max"], rank=clean["rank"],
         toolchain=clean["toolchain"], type_conf="manual", type_locked=1, details_ok=1,
         manual=1, source="manual", notes=clean["notes"],
         status="ready" if clean["scope"] else "needs-details",
         cond_ok=1 if clean["scope"] else 0, targets_n=len(clean["scope"]))
    if clean["scope"]:
        contract.save_conditions(pid, **manual.conditions_from(clean))
    log(pid, "manual: saved %d asset(s), %d tier(s), poc %s"
        % (len(clean["scope"]), len(clean["tiers"]), clean["poc"]))


def manual_update(pid, d):
    clean, errs = manual.validate(d, draft=bool(d.get("draft")))
    if errs:
        return errs
    apply_manual(pid, clean)
    return []


def manual_load(pid):
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return {}
    pol = load_conditions(pid)
    return {"name": p["name"], "url": p["url"], "toolchain": p["toolchain"], "max": p["max"],
            "rank": p["rank"], "notes": p["notes"] or "",
            "scope": [t.get("address") for t in (pol.get("targets") or [])],
            "tiers": [{"tier": r.get("tier"),
                       "max": int(re.sub(r"\D", "", r.get("band") or "0") or 0)}
                      for r in (pol.get("severity") or [])],
            "poc": bool((pol.get("poc") or {}).get("required")),
            "channel": (pol.get("submit") or {}).get("channel") or "",
            "address": (pol.get("submit") or {}).get("address") or "",
            "exclusions": (pol.get("exclusions") or {}).get("surfaces") or [],
            "template": pol.get("template") or []}


def manual_rows():
    with db() as c:
        return c.execute("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE manual=1 ORDER BY updated DESC").fetchall()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- plan / state
def plan(pid):
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return {"levels": []}
    tc = p["toolchain"]
    pol = load_conditions(pid)
    levels = []
    for lvl in range(6):
        sel = [k for k, v in TOOLS.items()
               if v["lvl"] == lvl and (not v["langs"] or tc in v["langs"] or tc == "unknown")]
        if FUND_ONLY[0]:
            sel = [k for k in sel if TOOLS[k]["fund"]]
        if lvl == 5:
            sel = sel[:HAMMER_DEPTH]
        levels.append({"level": lvl,
                       "name": ["recon", "static", "symbolic", "dynamic", "adversarial",
                                "hammer"][lvl],
                       "count": len(sel), "tools": sel, "applicable": lvl == 0 or bool(sel),
                       "note": "" if sel else "no applicable methods"})
    return {"levels": levels, "label": tc, "scope_model": pol.get("scope_model", "unknown"),
            "poc_required": bool((pol.get("poc") or {}).get("required")),
            "threshold": THRESHOLD, "details_ok": bool(p["details_ok"]), "web_target": ""}


def state():
    with db() as c:
        rows = c.execute("SELECT * FROM projects ORDER BY rank DESC, id DESC").fetchall()
        hits = {r["pid"]: r["n"] for r in
                c.execute("SELECT pid, COUNT(*) n FROM findings GROUP BY pid").fetchall()}
        unj = {r["pid"]: r["n"] for r in c.execute(
            "SELECT pid, COUNT(*) n FROM findings WHERE judged=0 GROUP BY pid").fetchall()}
        rdy = {r["pid"]: r["n"] for r in c.execute(
            "SELECT pid, COUNT(*) n FROM findings WHERE eligible=1 GROUP BY pid").fetchall()}
        reps = {r["pid"]: r["n"] for r in c.execute(
            "SELECT pid, COUNT(*) n FROM repairs GROUP BY pid").fetchall()}
        tally = {s: 0 for s in SEV_ORDER}
        for r in c.execute("SELECT sev, COUNT(*) n FROM findings GROUP BY sev").fetchall():
            tally[r["sev"]] = r["n"]
    live = running_pids()
    projects = []
    for r in rows:
        d = dict(r)
        d["hits"] = hits.get(r["id"], 0)
        d["unjudged"] = unj.get(r["id"], 0)
        d["ready"] = rdy.get(r["id"], 0)
        d["repairs"] = reps.get(r["id"], 0)
        d["live"] = r["id"] in live
        d["web"] = r["toolchain"] == "web"
        projects.append(d)
    return {"projects": projects, "tally": tally, "running": len(live), "workers": WORKERS,
            "tools": len(TOOLS), "threshold": THRESHOLD, "fund_only": FUND_ONLY[0],
            "stopped": STOP.is_set(), "auto_discover": AUTO_DISCOVER,
            "fund": sum(d["fund_hits"] for d in projects), "repair_kinds": REPAIR_KINDS}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- http
class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    server_version = "bountyhunter/10"

    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass

    def _j(self, obj, code=200):
        b = json.dumps(obj, default=str).encode()
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(b)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(b)
        self.close_connection = True

    def _h(self, body, ctype="text/html; charset=utf-8"):
        b = body.encode() if isinstance(body, str) else body
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(b)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(b)
        self.close_connection = True

    def _body(self):
        n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
        if not n:
            return {}
        try:
            return json.loads(self.rfile.read(n) or b"{}")
        except Exception:
            return {}

    def do_GET(self):
        self.route("GET")

    def do_POST(self):
        self.route("POST")

    def route(self, m):
        global AUTO_DISCOVER
        u = urlparse(self.path)
        q = {k: v[0] for k, v in parse_qs(u.query).items()}
        try:
            if u.path in ("/", "/index.html"):
                return self._h(UI.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if u.path == "/api/state":
                return self._j(state())
            if u.path == "/api/health":
                with db() as c:
                    t0 = time.time()
                    c.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()
                    dbms = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
                with RUN_LOCK:
                    live = sorted(RUNNING.keys())
                return self._j({"uptime": int(time.time() - STARTED[0]), "queue": JOBQ.qsize(),
                                "live": live, "db_ms": dbms,
                                "net_penalty": round(NET_PENALTY[0], 1),
                                "threads": threading.active_count(),
                                "discover": AUTO_DISCOVER, "discovering": NET_DISC.is_set(),
                                "workers": WORKERS})
            if u.path == "/api/log":
                pid = int(q.get("pid") or 0)
                since = int(q.get("since") or 0)
                with db() as c:
                    rows = c.execute("SELECT id,ts,line FROM log WHERE id>? AND (?=0 OR pid=?) "
                                     "ORDER BY id LIMIT 400", (since, pid, pid)).fetchall()
                return self._j([dict(r) for r in rows])
            if u.path == "/api/findings":
                return self._j([dict(r) for r in findings(int(q.get("pid") or 0))])
            if u.path == "/api/report":
                f = project_dir(int(q.get("pid") or 0)) / "report.md"
                return self._j({"report": f.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if f.exists() else ""})
            if u.path == "/api/brief" or u.path == "/api/conditions":
                return self._j(load_conditions(int(q.get("pid") or 0)))
            if u.path == "/api/readiness":
                pid = int(q.get("pid") or 0)
                p = getp(pid)
                rows = findings(pid)
                return self._j({"score": p["readiness"] if p else 0,
                                "ready": sum(1 for f in rows if f["eligible"]),
                                "total": len(rows), "requirements": []})
            if u.path == "/api/plan":
                return self._j(plan(int(q.get("pid") or 0)))
            if u.path == "/api/value":
                pid = int(q.get("pid") or 0)
                f = project_dir(pid) / "value.txt"
                p = getp(pid)
                return self._j({"value": f.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if f.exists() else "",
                                "tvl": p["tvl"] if p else 0})
            if u.path == "/api/details":
                pid = int(q.get("pid") or 0)
                f = project_dir(pid) / "details.json"
                d = {}
                if f.exists():
                    try:
                        d = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                    except Exception:
                        d = {}
                return self._j({"details": d, "reason": d.get("reason", "")})
            if u.path == "/api/probe":
                p = getp(int(q.get("pid") or 0))
                return self._j({"adapter": "manual" if p and p["manual"] else "generic",
                                "url_ok": bool(p and p["url"].startswith("http")),
                                "url_reason": "" if p and p["url"].startswith("http")
                                else "not an http url", "api_candidates": []})
            if u.path == "/api/discovery_audit":
                with db() as c:
                    rows = c.execute("SELECT url,why FROM rejections ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200"
                                     ).fetchall()
                return self._j([dict(r) for r in rows])
            if u.path == "/api/catalog":
                return self._j([{"name": n, "url": x, "toolchain": t, "max": mx}
                                for n, x, t, mx in platforms.SEEDS])
            if u.path == "/api/tools":
                return self._j([{"id": k, **v} for k, v in TOOLS.items()])
            if u.path == "/api/repairs":
                with db() as c:
                    rows = c.execute("SELECT * FROM repairs ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200").fetchall()
                return self._j([dict(r) for r in rows])
            if u.path == "/api/discover":
                return self._j({"added": discover()})
            if u.path == "/api/manual/presets":
                return self._j(manual.presets())
            if u.path == "/api/manual":
                pid = int(q.get("pid") or 0)
                if pid:
                    return self._j(manual_load(pid))
                return self._j({"manual": [dict(r) for r in manual_rows()]})

            if m != "POST":
                return self._j({"msg": "method not allowed"}, 405)
            b = self._body()

            if u.path == "/api/add":
                return self._j({"id": add_project(b.get("url", ""), b.get("name", ""))})
            if u.path == "/api/manual":
                pid = int(b.get("pid") or 0)
                if pid:
                    errs = manual_update(pid, b)
                    return self._j({"id": pid, "errors": errs}, 400 if errs else 200)
                pid, errs = manual_create(b)
                if errs:
                    return self._j({"msg": errs[0], "errors": errs}, 400)
                return self._j({"id": pid})
            if u.path == "/api/manual/bulk":
                made, bad = manual.bulk(b.get("text", ""))
                ids = []
                for c in made:
                    pid, errs = manual_create(c)
                    if pid:
                        ids.append(pid)
                return self._j({"added": len(ids), "rejected": bad})
            if u.path == "/api/manual/parse":
                return self._j(manual.parse_block(b.get("text", "")))
            if u.path == "/api/manual/clear":
                pid = int(b.get("pid") or 0)
                setp(pid, manual=0, source="auto", type_locked=0, details_ok=0)
                log(pid, "manual: released to discovery")
                return self._j({"ok": True})
            if u.path == "/api/paste":
                pid = int(b.get("pid") or 0)
                pol = contract.parse_program(pid, project_dir(pid), text=b.get("text", ""))
                contract.save_conditions(pid, **pol)
                setp(pid, cond_ok=1, targets_n=len(pol.get("targets") or []))
                return self._j({"ok": bool(pol.get("targets")),
                                "assets": len(pol.get("targets") or []),
                                "tiers": len(pol.get("severity") or [])})
            if u.path == "/api/purge":
                pid = int(b.get("pid") or 0)
                with db() as c:
                    if pid:
                        c.execute("DELETE FROM findings WHERE pid=?", (pid,))
                        c.execute("DELETE FROM log WHERE pid=?", (pid,))
                    else:
                        c.execute("DELETE FROM findings")
                        c.execute("DELETE FROM log WHERE pid>0")
                return self._j({"ok": True})
            if u.path == "/api/restart":
                pid = int(b.get("pid") or 0)
                with db() as c:
                    c.execute("DELETE FROM findings WHERE pid=?", (pid,))
                    c.execute("UPDATE projects SET status='new', stage='', escalation=0, "
                              "attempts=0, cycle=0, readiness=0, cond_ok=0 WHERE id=?", (pid,))
                shutil.rmtree(project_dir(pid), ignore_errors=True)
                queue_job(pid)
                return self._j({"ok": True})
            if u.path == "/api/attack" or u.path == "/api/run":
                return self._j({"ok": queue_job(int(b.get("pid") or 0))})
            if u.path == "/api/stop":
                pid = int(b.get("pid") or 0)
                if pid:
                    with RUN_LOCK:
                        RUNNING.pop(pid, None)
                else:
                    STOP.set()
                    time.sleep(0.25)
                    STOP.clear()
                return self._j({"ok": True})
            if u.path == "/api/start_all":
                with db() as c:
                    for r in c.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE auto=1").fetchall():
                        queue_job(r["id"])
                return self._j({"ok": True})
            if u.path == "/api/reset":
                STOP.clear()
                return self._j({"ok": True})
            if u.path == "/api/auto":
                setp(int(b.get("pid") or 0), auto=1 if b.get("on") else 0)
                return self._j({"ok": True})
            if u.path == "/api/autodiscover":
                AUTO_DISCOVER = bool(b.get("on"))
                return self._j({"on": AUTO_DISCOVER})
            if u.path == "/api/fundonly":
                FUND_ONLY[0] = bool(b.get("on"))
                return self._j({"on": FUND_ONLY[0]})
            if u.path == "/api/type":
                setp(int(b.get("pid") or 0), toolchain=b.get("toolchain", "unknown"),
                     type_locked=1, type_conf="manual")
                return self._j({"ok": True})
            if u.path == "/api/tool":
                run_tool(int(b.get("pid") or 0), b.get("id", ""))
                return self._j({"ok": True})
            if u.path == "/api/poc":
                step_poc({"pid": int(b.get("pid") or 0)})
                return self._j({"written": 1})
            if u.path == "/api/repair":
                repair(int(b.get("pid") or 0), b.get("kind", "locked_files"))
                return self._j({"ok": True})
            return self._j({"msg": "not found"}, 404)
        except Exception as ex:
            traceback.print_exc()
            return self._j({"msg": "%s: %s" % (type(ex).__name__, ex)}, 500)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- boot
def serve():
    for port in PORTS:
        try:
            ThreadingHTTPServer.allow_reuse_address = True
            srv = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), H)
        except OSError:
            continue
        srv.daemon_threads = True
        log(0, "ui: http://127.0.0.1:%d" % port)
        for fn in (log_writer, heartbeat, discover_loop, autopilot):
            threading.Thread(target=fn, daemon=True).start()
        for _ in range(WORKERS):
            threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True).start()
        threading.Thread(target=lambda u: webbrowser.open(u),
                         args=("http://127.0.0.1:%d" % port,), daemon=True).start()
        srv.serve_forever()
        return
    log(0, "no free port")


def main():
    init()
    STOP.clear()
    with db() as c:
        n = c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM projects").fetchone()["n"]
    log(0, "BountyHunter 10 - %d methods, %d fund, %d projects, %d manual"
        % (len(TOOLS), len(FUND), n, len(manual_rows())))
    log(0, "chain: %d rpc, etherscan %s, eth $%.0f, census %s"
        % (len([v for v in RPC.values() if v]), "set" if ETHERSCAN else "off", ETH_USD,
           "on" if VALUE_CENSUS else "off"))
    log(0, "gates: threshold %d, attempts %d, repairs %d, cycles %d, hammer %d"
        % (THRESHOLD, MAX_ATTEMPTS, MAX_REPAIRS, MAX_CYCLES, HAMMER_DEPTH))
    if DUPES:
        log(0, "env conflicts: %s" % ", ".join(sorted(set(DUPES))))
    serve()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
manual.py
python
"""Manual program entry: presets, parsing, validation, normalisation."""
import re

KNOWN = {"solidity", "rust", "go", "python", "js", "php", "ruby", "java", "c", "web", "unknown"}

PRESETS = {
    "evm": {"label": "EVM / Solidity", "toolchain": "solidity", "poc": True,
            "tiers": [("critical", 1000000), ("high", 50000), ("medium", 10000), ("low", 1000)],
            "template": ["summary", "affected", "impact", "likelihood", "poc", "fix"],
            "submit": "email"},
    "solana": {"label": "Solana / Rust", "toolchain": "rust", "poc": True,
               "tiers": [("critical", 500000), ("high", 25000), ("medium", 5000), ("low", 500)],
               "template": ["summary", "affected", "impact", "poc", "fix"], "submit": "email"},
    "web": {"label": "Web app / API", "toolchain": "web", "poc": False,
            "tiers": [("critical", 20000), ("high", 5000), ("medium", 1000), ("low", 200)],
            "template": ["summary", "steps", "impact", "fix"], "submit": "form"},
    "generic": {"label": "Generic source repo", "toolchain": "unknown", "poc": False,
                "tiers": [("critical", 10000), ("high", 2000), ("medium", 500), ("low", 100)],
                "template": ["summary", "affected", "impact", "fix"], "submit": "email"},
}

ADDR = re.compile(r"\b0x[a-fA-F0-9]{40}\b")
SOL = re.compile(r"\b[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,44}\b")
REPO = re.compile(r"\b(?:https?://)?(?:www\.)?github\.com/([\w.-]+)/([\w.-]+)")
DOMAIN = re.compile(r"\b(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z]{2,24}\b", re.I)
TIERLINE = re.compile(r"\b(critical|high|medium|low|informational|info)\b[^\n$]{0,60}?"
                      r"\$\s?([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*([kKmM])?", re.I)
EMAIL = re.compile(r"\b[\w.+-]+@[\w-]+\.[\w.]{2,}\b")
EXCL_HEAD = re.compile(r"out of scope|exclusion|not in scope|non-?vuln|non-?submission", re.I)
SCALE = {"k": 1_000, "m": 1_000_000}


def presets():
    return [{"id": k, "label": v["label"], "toolchain": v["toolchain"], "poc": v["poc"],
             "tiers": [{"tier": t, "max": m} for t, m in v["tiers"]],
             "template": v["template"], "submit": v["submit"]} for k, v in PRESETS.items()]


def _money(num, suf):
    try:
        v = float(num.replace(",", ""))
    except ValueError:
        return 0
    return int(v * SCALE.get((suf or "").lower(), 1))


def parse_block(text):
    """Best-effort draft from pasted program text. Never raises."""
    text = text or ""
    d = {"name": "", "url": "", "toolchain": "unknown", "max": 0, "rank": 60, "scope": [],
         "tiers": [], "poc": False, "channel": "", "address": "", "exclusions": [],
         "template": [], "notes": ""}
    for line in text.splitlines()[:12]:
        s = line.strip()
        if s and not d["name"] and 2 <= len(s) <= 80 and not s.lower().startswith("http"):
            d["name"] = s
            break
    m = re.search(r"https?://[^\s)>\]]+", text)
    if m:
        d["url"] = m.group(0).rstrip(".,;")
    r = REPO.search(text)
    if r:
        d["scope"].append("https://github.com/%s/%s" % (r.group(1), r.group(2).rstrip(".")))
        d["toolchain"] = "rust"
    for a in ADDR.findall(text):
        if a not in d["scope"]:
            d["scope"].append(a)
    if not d["scope"]:
        for s in SOL.findall(text):
            if len(s) >= 32 and not s.startswith("0x") and s not in d["scope"]:
                d["scope"].append(s)
                d["toolchain"] = "rust"
    low = text.lower()
    if re.search(r"\bsolidity|smart contract|\bevm\b|erc-?20", low) and d["toolchain"] == "unknown":
        d["toolchain"] = "solidity"
    if re.search(r"\banchor\b|\bsolana\b|\bpda\b", low):
        d["toolchain"] = "rust"
    if not d["scope"]:
        for h in DOMAIN.findall(text):
            h = h.lower()
            if h.endswith((".png", ".jpg", ".svg", ".js", ".css")):
                continue
            d["scope"].append(h)
            if d["toolchain"] == "unknown" and not re.match(r"^docs?\.", h):
                d["toolchain"] = "web"
            if len(d["scope"]) >= 6:
                break
    for m2 in TIERLINE.finditer(text):
        d["tiers"].append((m2.group(1).lower().replace("informational", "info"),
                           _money(m2.group(2), m2.group(3))))
    if d["tiers"]:
        d["max"] = max(v for _, v in d["tiers"])
    if re.search(r"\b(poc|proof of concept)\b[^\n]{0,40}\b(required|must|mandatory)\b", low):
        d["poc"] = True
    e = EMAIL.search(text)
    if e:
        d["channel"], d["address"] = "email", e.group(0)
    elif re.search(r"\bsubmit[^\n]{0,40}\bform\b", low):
        d["channel"] = "form"
    grab = False
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if EXCL_HEAD.search(line):
            grab = True
            continue
        if grab:
            s = line.strip(" -*\t")
            if not s:
                if d["exclusions"]:
                    grab = False
                continue
            if len(s) < 120:
                d["exclusions"].append(s)
            if len(d["exclusions"]) >= 25:
                break
    p = PRESETS.get(d["toolchain"] if d["toolchain"] in PRESETS else "generic")
    d["template"] = list(p["template"])
    return d


def _scope(raw):
    out = []
    for item in raw if isinstance(raw, list) else str(raw or "").splitlines():
        s = str(item).strip().strip(",")
        if not s:
            continue
        r = REPO.search(s)
        if r:
            s = "https://github.com/%s/%s" % (r.group(1), r.group(2).rstrip("."))
        if s not in out:
            out.append(s)
    return out[:300]


def _tiers(raw):
    out = []
    for row in raw or []:
        if isinstance(row, dict):
            tier, mx = row.get("tier"), row.get("max")
        else:
            pair = list(row) + [0]
            tier, mx = pair[0], pair[1]
        tier = str(tier or "").lower().replace("informational", "info")
        if tier not in ("critical", "high", "medium", "low", "info"):
            continue
        try:
            mx = max(0, min(10_000_000, int(float(mx or 0))))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            mx = 0
        if tier not in [t for t, _ in out]:
            out.append((tier, mx))
    return out


def validate(d, draft=False):
    """Return (clean, errors)."""
    errs = []
    name = str(d.get("name") or "").strip()[:80]
    if len(name) < 2:
        errs.append("name needs at least 2 characters")
    url = str(d.get("url") or "").strip()[:400]
    if url and not re.match(r"^https?://", url) and not REPO.search(url) \
            and not ADDR.fullmatch(url) and not SOL.fullmatch(url):
        errs.append("url must be http(s), a github repo, or an address")
    toolchain = str(d.get("toolchain") or "unknown").lower()
    if toolchain not in KNOWN:
        toolchain = "unknown"
    try:
        mx = max(0, min(10_000_000, int(float(d.get("max") or 0))))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        mx = 0
        errs.append("max bounty must be a number")
    try:
        rank = max(1, min(100, int(float(d.get("rank") or 60))))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        rank = 60
    scope = _scope(d.get("scope"))
    tiers = _tiers(d.get("tiers"))
    if not draft and not scope:
        errs.append("at least one scope asset is required")
    poc = d.get("poc")
    if isinstance(poc, str):
        poc = poc.strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")
    poc = bool(poc) or toolchain in ("solidity", "rust")
    channel = str(d.get("channel") or "").strip().lower()
    if channel not in ("email", "form", "platform", "other", ""):
        channel = "other"
    address = str(d.get("address") or "").strip()[:300]
    if channel == "email" and address and not EMAIL.search(address):
        errs.append("submission address is not an email")
    exclusions = [str(s).strip()[:200] for s in (d.get("exclusions") or []) if str(s).strip()][:60]
    template = [str(s).strip()[:40] for s in (d.get("template") or []) if str(s).strip()][:12]
    return {"name": name, "url": url, "toolchain": toolchain, "max": mx, "rank": rank,
            "scope": scope, "tiers": tiers, "poc": poc, "channel": channel,
            "address": address, "exclusions": exclusions, "template": template,
            "notes": str(d.get("notes") or "").strip()[:4000], "draft": bool(draft)}, errs


def slugify(name, pid=0):
    s = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", (name or "").lower()).strip("-")[:48]
    return "%s-%d" % (s or "manual", pid)


def conditions_from(c):
    return {"scope_model": "manual",
            "targets": [{"address": a, "chain": "manual", "kind": "manual"}
                        for a in c["scope"]],
            "severity": [{"tier": t, "band": ("<=$%d" % m) if m else "", "definition": ""}
                         for t, m in c["tiers"]],
            "poc": {"required": c["poc"], "framework": "", "source": "manual"},
            "exclusions": {"surfaces": c["exclusions"], "non_vulns": [],
                           "non_submissions": []},
            "submit": {"channel": c["channel"], "address": c["address"], "subject_fmt": ""},
            "template": c["template"], "one_page": False, "constraints": {}}


def bulk(text):
    """name | url | type | max | asset,asset"""
    made, bad = [], []
    for i, line in enumerate((text or "").splitlines(), 1):
        line = line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            continue
        parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split("|")]
        while len(parts) < 5:
            parts.append("")
        clean, errs = validate({"name": parts[0], "url": parts[1],
                                "toolchain": parts[2] or "unknown", "max": parts[3],
                                "scope": [s for s in re.split(r"[,\s]+", parts[4]) if s]})
        if errs:
            bad.append((i, line[:60], "; ".join(errs)))
        else:
            made.append(clean)
    return made, bad
platforms.py
python
"""Sources, detail adapters and the type resolver."""
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

UA = "Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; bountyhunter/10)"

SEEDS = [
    ("tori", "https://docs.tori.finance/security/audits", "solidity", 1000000),
    ("aladdin", "https://docs.aladdin.club/security-bounty", "solidity", 500000),
    ("rigoblock", "https://docs.rigoblock.com/bug-bounty", "solidity", 10000),
    ("hackenproof", "https://hackenproof.com/programs", "web", 50000),
    ("immunefi", "https://immunefi.com/bug-bounty/", "solidity", 1000000),
    ("bugcrowd", "https://bugcrowd.com/programs", "web", 30000),
    ("cantina", "https://cantina.xyz/competitions", "solidity", 200000),
    ("sherlock", "https://audits.sherlock.xyz/contests", "solidity", 100000),
    ("code4rena", "https://code4rena.com/audits", "solidity", 100000),
    ("hats", "https://hats.finance/", "solidity", 50000),
    ("huntr", "https://huntr.com/bounties", "python", 5000),
    ("intigriti", "https://www.intigriti.com/programs", "web", 20000),
]


def _host(url):
    return re.sub(r"^https?://", "", url).split("/")[0]


def _get(url, timeout=30):
    import urllib.request
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": UA})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
        return r.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")


def collect_all():
    progs, audits, pins, rej = [], [], [], []
    for name, url, tc, mx in SEEDS:
        try:
            body = _get(url)
        except Exception as ex:
            rej.append((url, str(ex)[:80]))
            continue
        if not body:
            rej.append((url, "empty body"))
            continue
        found = 0
        for m in re.finditer(r'href="(/(?:programs|program|bounties|contests|audits)/[\w./-]+)"',
                             body):
            u = "https://" + _host(url) + m.group(1)
            if u not in [p[1] for p in progs]:
                progs.append((name + "-" + m.group(1).rsplit("/", 1)[-1][:24], u, tc, mx))
                found += 1
            if found >= 6:
                break
        if not found:
            audits.append((name, url))
    return progs, audits, pins, rej, [], [], []


def details(url, d):
    body = _get(url)
    (Path(d) / "details.html").write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
    (Path(d) / "details.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({"url": url, "bytes": len(body), "adapter": "generic", "tier": "html",
                    "reason": ""}), encoding="utf-8")
    return True


def resolve_type(d, url):
    p = Path(d)
    for name, tc in (("Cargo.toml", "rust"), ("foundry.toml", "solidity"),
                     ("hardhat.config.js", "solidity"), ("go.mod", "go"),
                     ("requirements.txt", "python"), ("composer.json", "php"),
                     ("Gemfile", "ruby"), ("pom.xml", "java"), ("package.json", "js")):
        if (p / name).exists():
            return tc, "manifest", {"reason": name, "manifest": [name]}
    if any(p.glob("**/*.sol")):
        return "solidity", "census", {"reason": "sol files"}
    if "web" in url:
        return "web", "address", {"reason": "web url"}
    return "unknown", "unresolved", {"reason": "no evidence"}
contract.py
python
"""Program contract: parse a page or pasted text into scope, tiers, poc, submit."""
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

import manual


def parse_program(pid_or_url, d=None, text=""):
    if not text and d:
        f = Path(d) / "details.html"
        if f.exists():
            text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore"))
    clean, _ = manual.validate(manual.parse_block(text), draft=True)
    return manual.conditions_from(clean)


def save_conditions(pid, **pol):
    import bh
    f = bh.project_dir(pid) / "contract.json"
    f.write_text(json.dumps(pol, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return f
selftest.py
python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
for f in ("bh.py", "platforms.py", "contract.py", "manual.py", "ui.html"):
    if not (ROOT / f).exists():
        sys.exit("missing: " + f)
try:
    import bh
except Exception as ex:
    sys.exit("import failed: %s: %s" % (type(ex).__name__, ex))

bad = []
for n in ("intake", "conditions", "brief", "gate", "report", "poc"):
    if n not in bh.STEP_FN or not callable(bh.STEP_FN[n]):
        bad.append("step " + n)
for k, v in bh.TOOLS.items():
    if not v.get("name") or not v.get("cls"):
        bad.append("tool " + k)
if len(bh.TOOLS) < 100:
    bad.append("tool count %d" % len(bh.TOOLS))
src = (ROOT / "bh.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
for r in ("/api/state", "/api/manual", "/api/manual/parse", "/api/health", "/api/attack"):
    if r not in src:
        bad.append("route " + r)
ui = (ROOT / "ui.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
for n in ("/api/manual", "/api/state", "render", "openManual"):
    if n not in ui:
        bad.append("ui missing " + n)

envf = ROOT / ".env"
if not envf.exists():
    bad.append(".env missing")
else:
    keys = [l.split("=", 1)[0].strip()
            for l in envf.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines()
            if "=" in l and not l.strip().startswith("#")]
    dup = {k for k in keys if keys.count(k) > 1}
    if dup:
        bad.append("duplicate .env keys: " + ", ".join(sorted(dup)))
    for k in ("ETH_RPC_URL", "RPC_BASE", "ETHERSCAN_KEY", "GITHUB_TOKEN", "BH_THRESHOLD"):
        if k not in keys:
            bad.append(".env missing " + k)

if bad:
    print("SELFTEST FAIL")
    for b in bad:
        print("  -", b)
    sys.exit(1)
print("selftest ok: %d steps, %d methods, %d fund" % (len(bh.STEP_FN), len(bh.TOOLS),
                                                      len(bh.FUND)))
reset.py
python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

DB = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "bh.db"
c = sqlite3.connect(DB)
c.row_factory = sqlite3.Row

NEED = {
    "projects": [("url", "TEXT"), ("slug", "TEXT"), ("name", "TEXT"), ("status", "TEXT"),
                 ("stage", "TEXT"), ("cycle", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                 ("attempts", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("escalation", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                 ("toolchain", "TEXT DEFAULT 'unknown'"),
                 ("type_conf", "TEXT DEFAULT 'unresolved'"),
                 ("type_evidence", "TEXT DEFAULT '{}'"), ("type_locked", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                 ("max", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("rank", "INTEGER DEFAULT 50"),
                 ("tvl", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("details_ok", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                 ("cond_ok", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("targets_n", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                 ("readiness", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("fund_hits", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                 ("auto", "INTEGER DEFAULT 1"), ("source", "TEXT DEFAULT 'auto'"),
                 ("manual", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("notes", "TEXT DEFAULT ''"),
                 ("added", "REAL"), ("updated", "REAL")],
    "findings": [("pid", "INTEGER"), ("title", "TEXT"), ("sev", "TEXT"), ("cls", "TEXT"),
                 ("file", "TEXT"), ("line", "INTEGER"), ("evidence", "TEXT"),
                 ("score", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("fund", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                 ("reward", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("gate", "TEXT DEFAULT ''"),
                 ("blockers", "TEXT DEFAULT '[]'"), ("judged", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                 ("eligible", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("poc_path", "TEXT DEFAULT ''"),
                 ("tool", "TEXT"), ("ts", "REAL")],
    "log": [("pid", "INTEGER"), ("ts", "REAL"), ("line", "TEXT")],
    "repairs": [("pid", "INTEGER"), ("kind", "TEXT"), ("detail", "TEXT"), ("ts", "REAL")],
    "rejections": [("url", "TEXT"), ("why", "TEXT"), ("ts", "REAL")],
}

for t, cols in NEED.items():
    c.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS %s(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT)" % t)
    have = {r["name"] for r in c.execute("PRAGMA table_info(%s)" % t)}
    for n, d in cols:
        if n not in have:
            c.execute("ALTER TABLE %s ADD COLUMN %s %s" % (t, n, d))
c.commit()
print("reset ok: %d projects" % c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM projects").fetchone()[0])
dump.py
python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import bh, contract

if len(sys.argv) < 2:
    sys.exit("usage: dump.py <url-or-file>")
arg = sys.argv[1]
text = Path(arg).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") if Path(arg).exists() else ""
pol = contract.parse_program(arg, None, text=text)
for k in ("scope_model", "targets", "severity", "poc", "exclusions", "submit", "template"):
    print("\n== %s ==" % k)
    print(pol.get(k))
print("\n== methods ==\n%d registered, %d fund" % (len(bh.TOOLS), len(bh.FUND)))
run.bat
bat
@echo off
setlocal enableextensions
chcp 65001 >nul
cd /d "%~dp0"
set "PY="
where py >nul 2>nul && set "PY=py -3"
if not defined PY (where python >nul 2>nul && set "PY=python")
if not defined PY (echo [x] python not on PATH & pause & exit /b 1)
for %%F in (bh.py platforms.py contract.py manual.py ui.html selftest.py) do (
  if not exist "%%F" (echo [x] %%F missing & pause & exit /b 1)
)
if not exist .env (echo [x] .env missing & pause & exit /b 1)
%PY% -m py_compile bh.py || (echo [x] bh.py does not parse & pause & exit /b 1)
%PY% -m py_compile manual.py || (echo [x] manual.py does not parse & pause & exit /b 1)
%PY% -m py_compile contract.py || (echo [x] contract.py does not parse & pause & exit /b 1)
%PY% -m py_compile platforms.py || (echo [x] platforms.py does not parse & pause & exit /b 1)
%PY% selftest.py || (echo [x] selftest failed & pause & exit /b 1)
echo [i] starting
%PY% bh.py
pause

border-radius:6px;padding:7px 10px;font-size:12.5px}
.fld input:focus,.fld select:focus,.fld textarea:focus{border-color:var(--ac)}
.fld .hint{margin-top:3px;font-size:11px}
