def _build():
    tools = {}
    for i, line in enumerate([l for l in RAW.strip().splitlines() if l.strip()], 1):
        cls, name, sev, langs, lvl, fund = [p.strip() for p in line.split("|")]
        tools["m%03d" % i] = {"name": name, "cls": cls, "sev": sev,
                              "langs": [x for x in langs.split(",") if x], "lvl": int(lvl),
                              "fund": fund == "1",
                              "reward": REWARD.get(cls, 0) if fund == "1" else 0}
    return tools


TOOLS = _build()
FUND = {k: v for k, v in TOOLS.items() if v["fund"]}


def _cols(c, table):
    return {r["name"] for r in c.execute("PRAGMA table_info(%s)" % table)}


def init():
    WORK.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with db() as c:
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, url TEXT UNIQUE, slug TEXT, name TEXT,
            status TEXT, stage TEXT, cycle INTEGER DEFAULT 0, attempts INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            escalation INTEGER DEFAULT 0, toolchain TEXT DEFAULT 'unknown',
            type_conf TEXT DEFAULT 'unresolved', type_evidence TEXT DEFAULT '{}',
            type_locked INTEGER DEFAULT 0, max INTEGER DEFAULT 0, rank INTEGER DEFAULT 50,
            tvl INTEGER DEFAULT 0, details_ok INTEGER DEFAULT 0, cond_ok INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            targets_n INTEGER DEFAULT 0, readiness INTEGER DEFAULT 0, fund_hits INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            auto INTEGER DEFAULT 1, source TEXT DEFAULT 'auto', manual INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            notes TEXT DEFAULT '', added REAL, updated REAL)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS findings(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, pid INTEGER, title TEXT, sev TEXT,
            cls TEXT, file TEXT, line INTEGER, evidence TEXT, score INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            fund INTEGER DEFAULT 0, reward INTEGER DEFAULT 0, gate TEXT DEFAULT '',
            blockers TEXT DEFAULT '[]', judged INTEGER DEFAULT 0, eligible INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            poc_path TEXT DEFAULT '', tool TEXT, ts REAL)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS log(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, pid INTEGER, ts REAL, line TEXT)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS repairs(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, pid INTEGER, kind TEXT, detail TEXT, ts REAL)""")
        c.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rejections(
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, url TEXT, why TEXT, ts REAL)""")
        c.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS i_f ON findings(pid)")
        c.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS i_l ON log(pid, id)")
        want = {
            "projects": [("slug", "TEXT"), ("source", "TEXT DEFAULT 'auto'"),
                         ("manual", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("notes", "TEXT DEFAULT ''"),
                         ("added", "REAL"), ("updated", "REAL"),
                         ("type_locked", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                         ("type_evidence", "TEXT DEFAULT '{}'"),
                         ("targets_n", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0")],
            "findings": [("blockers", "TEXT DEFAULT '[]'"), ("judged", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                         ("eligible", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"), ("reward", "INTEGER DEFAULT 0"),
                         ("poc_path", "TEXT DEFAULT ''"), ("tool", "TEXT")],
        }
        for table, cols in want.items():
            have = _cols(c, table)
            for name, decl in cols:
                if name not in have:
                    c.execute("ALTER TABLE %s ADD COLUMN %s %s" % (table, name, decl))


def _conn():
    c = getattr(_LOCAL, "c", None)
    if c is None:
        c = sqlite3.connect(DB, timeout=15, isolation_level=None)
        c.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        c.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        c.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL")
        c.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=15000")
        _LOCAL.c = c
    return c


@contextmanager
def db():
    yield _conn()


def log(pid, line):
    line = str(line)[:4000]
    ts = time.time()
    slug = ""
    try:
        with db() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO log(pid,ts,line) VALUES(?,?,?)", (pid, ts, line))
            r = c.execute("SELECT slug FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()
            slug = (r["slug"] if r else "") or ""
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        LOGQ.put_nowait((slug, ts, line))
    except queue.Full:
        pass
    print("[%s] %s" % (pid, line), flush=True)


def log_writer():
    while not STOP.is_set():
        try:
            slug, ts, line = LOGQ.get(timeout=1.0)
        except queue.Empty:
            continue
        try:
            d = WORK / (slug or "0")
            d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with (d / "log.txt").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write("%s %s\n" % (time.strftime("%H:%M:%S", time.localtime(ts)), line))
        except Exception:
            pass


def heartbeat():
    while not STOP.is_set():
        time.sleep(20)
        try:
            with RUN_LOCK:
                live = sorted(RUNNING.keys())
            with db() as c:
                t0 = time.time()
                c.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()
                dbms = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
            log(0, "hb: %d live, queue %d, db %dms, net %.1fs, discover %s"
                % (len(live), JOBQ.qsize(), dbms, NET_PENALTY[0],
                   "on" if AUTO_DISCOVER else "off"))
        except Exception as ex:
            log(0, "hb: %s" % str(ex)[:120])


def _net_wait(host):
    gap = max(NET_GAP + NET_PENALTY[0], 0.0)
    end = time.time() + max(0.0, gap - (time.time() - max(NET_LAST[0],
                                                          HOST_LAST.get(host, 0.0))))
    while time.time() < end:
        if STOP.is_set():
            return
        time.sleep(min(0.2, end - time.time()))


def _penalise(code, retry=""):
    if code in (429, 503) or (code == 403 and retry):
        NET_PENALTY[0] = min(NET_MAX_PENALTY, max(NET_PENALTY[0] * 1.6, 1.5))
    else:
        NET_PENALTY[0] = max(0.0, NET_PENALTY[0] * 0.75)


def fetch(url, headers=None, timeout=45, data=None, method="GET"):
    host = urlparse(url).netloc
    with NET_LOCK:
        _net_wait(host)
        h = {"User-Agent": UA, "Accept": "*/*"}
        h.update(headers or {})
        code, body, hdrs = 0, "", {}
        try:
            import urllib.request
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=h, data=data, method=method)
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
                code = r.status
                body = r.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
                hdrs = dict(r.headers)
        except Exception as ex:
            code = getattr(ex, "code", 0) or 0
            if not code:
                raise
        finally:
            NET_LAST[0] = time.time()
            HOST_LAST[host] = NET_LAST[0]
            _penalise(code, hdrs.get("Retry-After", ""))
    if code >= 400:
        raise RuntimeError("HTTP %d" % code)
    return body


_SEL = {"totalAssets": "0x01e1d114", "totalSupply": "0x18160ddd", "decimals": "0x313ce567"}
_CHAIN = {}


def rpc(chain, method, params):
    url = RPC.get(chain)
    if not url:
        raise RuntimeError("no rpc for chain %s" % chain)
    body = json.dumps({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": method, "params": params}).encode()
    raw = fetch(url, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"}, data=body,
                method="POST", timeout=25)
    out = json.loads(raw)
    if "error" in out:
        raise RuntimeError(out["error"].get("message", "rpc error"))
    return out.get("result")


def chain_of(address):
    if address in _CHAIN:
        return _CHAIN[address]
    for chain in RPC:
        try:
            if rpc(chain, "eth_getCode", [address, "latest"]) not in (None, "0x"):
                _CHAIN[address] = chain
                return chain
        except Exception:
            continue
    return 0


def _uint(hexstr):
    if not hexstr or hexstr == "0x":
        return 0
    try:
        return int(hexstr, 16)
    except ValueError:
        return 0


def abi_of(chain, address):
    d = WORK / "abi"
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    f = d / ("%s-%s.json" % (chain, address))
    if f.exists():
        try:
            return json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            return []
    if not ETHERSCAN:
        return []
    try:
        raw = fetch("https://api.etherscan.io/v2/api?chainid=%d&module=contract&action=getabi"
                    "&address=%s&apikey=%s" % (chain, address, ETHERSCAN), timeout=25)
        j = json.loads(raw)
        abi = json.loads(j.get("result") or "[]") if j.get("status") == "1" else []
    except Exception:
        abi = []
    f.write_text(json.dumps(abi), encoding="utf-8")
    return abi


def fork_block(chain):
    if FORK_BLOCK:
        try:
            return int(FORK_BLOCK)
        except ValueError:
            pass
    try:
        latest = _uint(rpc(chain, "eth_blockNumber", []))
    except Exception:
        return 0
    return max(0, latest - FORK_OFFSET)


def census(pid):
    if not VALUE_CENSUS:
        return 0
    if not getp(pid):
        return 0
    targets = [t.get("address") for t in (load_conditions(pid).get("targets") or [])
               if t.get("address")]
    if not targets:
        return 0
    lines, total = [], 0
    for addr in targets[:40]:
        chain = chain_of(addr)
        if not chain:
            lines.append("%s  no code on configured chains" % addr)
            continue
        try:
            bal = _uint(rpc(chain, "eth_getBalance", [addr, "latest"]))
        except Exception as ex:
            lines.append("%s  balance error: %s" % (addr, ex))
            continue
        assets = supply = dec = 0
        names = {e.get("name") for e in abi_of(chain, addr) if e.get("type") == "function"}
        for fn in ("totalAssets", "totalSupply", "decimals"):
            if names and fn not in names:
                continue
            try:
                v = _uint(rpc(chain, "eth_call", [{"to": addr, "data": _SEL[fn]}, "latest"]))
            except Exception:
                v = 0
            if fn == "totalAssets":
                assets = v
            elif fn == "totalSupply":
                supply = v
            else:
                dec = v
        held = max(bal, assets)
        if supply and 0 < dec <= 36:
            held = max(held, supply * 10 ** (18 - dec) if dec < 18 else supply // 10 ** (dec - 18))
        total += held
        eth = held / 1e18
        lines.append("%s  chain %d  native %.6f  assets %.6f  supply %d  -> %.6f ETH  $%.0f"
                     % (addr, chain, bal / 1e18, assets / 1e18, supply, eth, eth * ETH_USD))
        fb = fork_block(chain)
        if fb:
            lines.append("    fork: chain %d block %d" % (chain, fb))
    (project_dir(pid) / "value.txt").write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
    setp(pid, tvl=total)
    log(pid, "census: %d target(s), %.6f ETH-eq, $%.0f"
        % (len(targets), total / 1e18, total / 1e18 * ETH_USD))
    return total


def add_project(url, name="", source="auto", toolchain="unknown", max_bounty=0, rank=50,
                manual_flag=0, details=0, locked=0):
    url = (url or "").strip()
    if not url:
        return 0
    with db() as c:
        r = c.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE url=?", (url,)).fetchone()
        if r:
            c.execute("UPDATE projects SET updated=?, auto=1 WHERE id=?", (time.time(), r["id"]))
            return r["id"]
        nm = name or urlparse(url).netloc or url
        cur = c.execute(
            "INSERT INTO projects(url,name,status,stage,toolchain,max,rank,source,manual,"
            "details_ok,type_locked,added,updated) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (url, nm[:80], "new", "", toolchain, max_bounty, rank, source, manual_flag,
             details, locked, time.time(), time.time()))
        pid = cur.lastrowid
        c.execute("UPDATE projects SET slug=? WHERE id=?", (manual.slugify(nm, pid), pid))
    log(pid, "queued %s" % url)
    return pid


def setp(pid, **kw):
    if not kw:
        return
    kw["updated"] = time.time()
    cols = ", ".join("%s=?" % k for k in kw)
    with db() as c:
        c.execute("UPDATE projects SET %s WHERE id=?" % cols, (*kw.values(), pid))


def getp(pid):
    with db() as c:
        return c.execute("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)).fetchone()


def running_pids():
    with RUN_LOCK:
        return set(RUNNING.keys())


def project_dir(pid):
    r = getp(pid)
    d = WORK / ((r["slug"] if r and r["slug"] else str(pid)))
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return d


def findings(pid):
    with db() as c:
        return c.execute("SELECT * FROM findings WHERE pid=? ORDER BY score DESC",
                         (pid,)).fetchall()


def load_conditions(pid):
    f = project_dir(pid) / "contract.json"
    if not f.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        return json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception:
        return {}


def _blockers(f, pol):
    b = []
    ex = pol.get("exclusions") or {}
    surf = " ".join(ex.get("surfaces") or []).lower()
    for word in ("cdn", "third-party", "testnet", "mock"):
        if word in surf and word in (f["file"] or "").lower():
            b.append("out of scope: " + word)
    if (pol.get("poc") or {}).get("required") and not f["poc_path"]:
        b.append("poc required")
    for nv in ex.get("non_vulns") or []:
        head = nv.lower()[:24]
        if head and head in (f["title"] or "").lower():
            b.append("known non-vuln")
            break
    return b


def gate(pid):
    if not getp(pid):
        return
    pol = load_conditions(pid)
    rows = findings(pid)
    eligible = money = 0
    for f in rows:
        bl = _blockers(f, pol)
        ok = not bl and f["score"] >= THRESHOLD and f["sev"] != "info"
        with db() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE findings SET blockers=?, judged=1, eligible=? WHERE id=?",
                      (json.dumps(bl), 1 if ok else 0, f["id"]))
        eligible += 1 if ok else 0
        money += 1 if (ok and f["fund"]) else 0
    score = int(100 * eligible / len(rows)) if rows else 0
    setp(pid, readiness=score, fund_hits=money)
    log(pid, "gate: %d findings, %d eligible, %d money, readiness %d"
        % (len(rows), eligible, money, score))


def step_intake(ctx):
    pid = ctx["pid"]
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return ctx
    if p["manual"]:
        ctx["details_ok"] = True
        log(pid, "intake: manual project, details gate bypassed")
    else:
        d = project_dir(pid) / "details.json"
        ok = d.exists() and d.stat().st_size > 200
        setp(pid, details_ok=1 if ok else 0, status="ready" if ok else "needs-details")
        ctx["details_ok"] = ok
        if not ok:
            log(pid, "intake: details missing, fetching")
            try:
                platforms.details(p["url"], project_dir(pid))
                setp(pid, details_ok=1, status="ready")
                ctx["details_ok"] = True
            except Exception as ex:
                log(pid, "intake: details failed: %s" % ex)
    if not p["type_locked"]:
        tc, conf, ev = platforms.resolve_type(project_dir(pid), p["url"])
        setp(pid, toolchain=tc, type_conf=conf, type_evidence=json.dumps(ev)[:4000])
        log(pid, "intake: type %s (%s)" % (tc, conf))
    return ctx


def step_conditions(ctx):
    pid = ctx["pid"]
    existing = load_conditions(pid)
    if existing:
        setp(pid, cond_ok=1, targets_n=len(existing.get("targets") or []))
        if VALUE_CENSUS and not (project_dir(pid) / "value.txt").exists():
            try:
                census(pid)
            except Exception as ex:
                log(pid, "census: %s" % ex)
        return ctx
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return ctx
    try:
        pol = contract.parse_program(p["url"], project_dir(pid))
        contract.save_conditions(pid, **pol)
        setp(pid, cond_ok=1, targets_n=len(pol.get("targets") or []))
        log(pid, "conditions: %d targets, %d tiers"
            % (len(pol.get("targets") or []), len(pol.get("severity") or [])))
        if VALUE_CENSUS:
            try:
                census(pid)
            except Exception as ex:
                log(pid, "census: %s" % ex)
    except Exception as ex:
        log(pid, "conditions: %s" % ex)
        setp(pid, cond_ok=0)
    return ctx


def step_brief(ctx):
    pid = ctx["pid"]
    pol = load_conditions(pid)
    ctx["brief"] = pol
    log(pid, "brief: scope %s, poc %s"
        % (pol.get("scope_model"), (pol.get("poc") or {}).get("required")))
    return ctx


def step_gate(ctx):
    gate(ctx["pid"])
    return ctx


def step_report(ctx):
    pid = ctx["pid"]
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return ctx
    rows = [f for f in findings(pid) if f["eligible"]]
    sub = load_conditions(pid).get("submit") or {}
    lines = ["# %s" % p["name"], "", "Target: %s" % p["url"], ""]
    for f in rows:
        lines += ["## %s - %s" % (f["sev"].upper(), f["title"]), "",
                  "Class: %s  |  Where: %s:%s  |  Score: %s"
                  % (f["cls"], f["file"], f["line"], f["score"]), "",
                  "```", (f["evidence"] or "")[:1500], "```", ""]
    (project_dir(pid) / "report.md").write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
    log(pid, "report: %d sections, channel %s" % (len(rows), sub.get("channel") or "unknown"))
    return ctx


def step_poc(ctx):
    pid = ctx["pid"]
    d = project_dir(pid) / "poc"
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    n = 0
    for f in findings(pid):
        if not f["eligible"] or f["poc_path"]:
            continue
        fn = d / ("%s.t.sol" % f["id"])
        first = (f["evidence"] or "repro").splitlines()[0][:120]
        fn.write_text(
            "// %s\n// %s:%s\npragma solidity ^0.8.20;\n\ncontract PoC_%d {\n"
            "    function test_exploit() public {\n        // %s\n    }\n}\n"
            % (f["title"], f["file"], f["line"], f["id"], first),
            encoding="utf-8")
        with db() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE findings SET poc_path=? WHERE id=?", (str(fn), f["id"]))
        n += 1
    log(pid, "poc: %d written" % n)
    return ctx


STEP_FN = {"intake": step_intake, "conditions": step_conditions, "brief": step_brief,
           "gate": step_gate, "report": step_report, "poc": step_poc}
STEPS = ["intake", "conditions", "brief", "gate", "report", "poc"]


def run_tool(pid, tool):
    t = TOOLS.get(tool)
    if not t:
        return ""
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(["echo", "%s: %s" % (tool, t["name"])],
                           cwd=str(project_dir(pid)), capture_output=True, text=True,
                           timeout=TOOL_TIMEOUT)
        out = (r.stdout or "") + (r.stderr or "")
    except Exception as ex:
        out = str(ex)
    log(pid, "%s: %d bytes" % (tool, len(out)))
    return out


def run_pipeline(pid):
    ctx = {"pid": pid}
    with RUN_LOCK:
        if pid in RUNNING:
            return
        RUNNING[pid] = time.time()
    try:
        p = getp(pid)
        if not p:
            return
        if p["cycle"] >= MAX_CYCLES and p["status"] in ("failed", "exhausted"):
            setp(pid, status="exhausted")
            log(pid, "cycles exhausted (%d)" % p["cycle"])
            return
        attempts = p["attempts"] + 1
        if attempts > MAX_ATTEMPTS:
            setp(pid, attempts=1, cycle=p["cycle"] + 1, escalation=min(5, p["escalation"] + 1))
            log(pid, "escalate to L%d, cycle %d/%d"
                % (min(5, p["escalation"] + 1), p["cycle"] + 1, MAX_CYCLES))
            time.sleep(CYCLE_SLEEP)
        else:
            setp(pid, attempts=attempts)
        setp(pid, status="running")
        for name in STEPS:
            if STOP.is_set():
                break
            fn = STEP_FN.get(name)
            if not fn:
                log(pid, "step missing: %s" % name)
                continue
            setp(pid, stage=name)
            try:
                ctx = fn(ctx) or ctx
            except Exception as ex:
                log(pid, "%s: %s" % (name, ex))
                if p["attempts"] <= MAX_REPAIRS:
                    repair(pid, "reset_readiness")
                setp(pid, status="failed")
        if getp(pid) and getp(pid)["status"] == "running":
            setp(pid, status="done", stage="")
    finally:
        with RUN_LOCK:
            RUNNING.pop(pid, None)


def worker():
    while not STOP.is_set():
        try:
            pid = JOBQ.get(timeout=1.0)
        except queue.Empty:
            continue
        if pid is None:
            return
        try:
            run_pipeline(pid)
        except Exception as ex:
            log(0, "worker: %s" % ex)


def queue_job(pid):
    try:
        JOBQ.put_nowait(pid)
        return True
    except queue.Full:
        log(0, "queue full, dropped %s" % pid)
        return False


def autopilot():
    while not STOP.is_set():
        time.sleep(3)
        with RUN_LOCK:
            busy = len(RUNNING)
        if busy >= WORKERS:
            continue
        with db() as c:
            rows = c.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE auto=1 AND status IN "
                             "('new','ready','failed') ORDER BY rank DESC LIMIT ?",
                             (WORKERS - busy,)).fetchall()
        for r in rows:
            queue_job(r["id"])


def getp_by_url(url):
    with db() as c:
        return c.execute("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE url=?", (url,)).fetchone()


def discover():
    t0 = time.time()
    added = 0
    try:
        prog, aud, pins, rej, _, _, _ = platforms.collect_all()
    except Exception as ex:
        log(0, "discover: %s" % ex)
        return 0
    for url, why in rej:
        with db() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO rejections(url,why,ts) VALUES(?,?,?)",
                      (url, why, time.time()))
    for name, url, tc, mx in prog[:DISCOVER_PIN]:
        if time.time() - t0 > DISCOVER_BUDGET:
            log(0, "discover: budget spent")
            break
        if getp_by_url(url):
            continue
        if add_project(url, name, source="discover", toolchain=tc, max_bounty=mx,
                       rank=min(90, 40 + mx // 5000)):
            added += 1
    return added


def discover_loop():
    time.sleep(8)
    while not STOP.is_set():
        if AUTO_DISCOVER and JOBQ.qsize() == 0 and not running_pids():
            NET_DISC.set()
            t0 = time.time()
            try:
                log(0, "discover: %d new (%.0fs)" % (discover(), time.time() - t0))
            except Exception as ex:
                log(0, "discover loop: %s" % ex)
            finally:
                NET_DISC.clear()
        for _ in range(DISCOVER_EVERY):
            if STOP.is_set():
                return
            time.sleep(1)


REPAIR_KINDS = ["locked_files", "thin_checkout", "bad_type", "reset_readiness"]


def repair(pid, kind):
    d = project_dir(pid)
    detail = "no-op"
    if kind == "locked_files":
        try:
            subprocess.run(["git", "config", "core.longpaths", "true"], cwd=str(d),
                           capture_output=True, timeout=30)
            detail = "longpaths set"
        except Exception as ex:
            detail = str(ex)
    elif kind == "thin_checkout":
        detail = "flagged for re-clone"
    elif kind == "bad_type":
        setp(pid, type_locked=0)
        detail = "type unlocked"
    elif kind == "reset_readiness":
        with db() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE findings SET eligible=0, blockers='[]' WHERE pid=?", (pid,))
        setp(pid, readiness=0)
        detail = "readiness cleared"
    with db() as c:
        c.execute("INSERT INTO repairs(pid,kind,detail,ts) VALUES(?,?,?,?)",
                  (pid, kind, detail, time.time()))
    log(pid, "repair %s: %s" % (kind, detail))


def manual_create(d):
    clean, errs = manual.validate(d, draft=bool(d.get("draft")))
    if errs:
        return 0, errs
    url = clean["url"] or "manual:%s" % manual.slugify(clean["name"], int(time.time()) % 100000)
    pid = add_project(url, clean["name"], source="manual", toolchain=clean["toolchain"],
                      max_bounty=clean["max"], rank=clean["rank"], manual_flag=1,
                      details=1, locked=1)
    if not pid:
        return 0, ["that url is already queued"]
    apply_manual(pid, clean)
    return pid, []


def apply_manual(pid, clean):
    setp(pid, name=clean["name"], max=clean["max"], rank=clean["rank"],
         toolchain=clean["toolchain"], type_conf="manual", type_locked=1, details_ok=1,
         manual=1, source="manual", notes=clean["notes"],
         status="ready" if clean["scope"] else "needs-details",
         cond_ok=1 if clean["scope"] else 0, targets_n=len(clean["scope"]))
    if clean["scope"]:
        contract.save_conditions(pid, **manual.conditions_from(clean))
    log(pid, "manual: saved %d asset(s), %d tier(s), poc %s"
        % (len(clean["scope"]), len(clean["tiers"]), clean["poc"]))


def manual_update(pid, d):
    clean, errs = manual.validate(d, draft=bool(d.get("draft")))
    if errs:
        return errs
    apply_manual(pid, clean)
    return []


def manual_load(pid):
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return {}
    pol = load_conditions(pid)
    return {"name": p["name"], "url": p["url"], "toolchain": p["toolchain"], "max": p["max"],
            "rank": p["rank"], "notes": p["notes"] or "",
            "scope": [t.get("address") for t in (pol.get("targets") or [])],
            "tiers": [{"tier": r.get("tier"),
                       "max": int(re.sub(r"\D", "", r.get("band") or "0") or 0)}
                      for r in (pol.get("severity") or [])],
            "poc": bool((pol.get("poc") or {}).get("required")),
            "channel": (pol.get("submit") or {}).get("channel") or "",
            "address": (pol.get("submit") or {}).get("address") or "",
            "exclusions": (pol.get("exclusions") or {}).get("surfaces") or [],
            "template": pol.get("template") or []}


def manual_rows():
    with db() as c:
        return c.execute("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE manual=1 ORDER BY updated DESC").fetchall()


def plan(pid):
    p = getp(pid)
    if not p:
        return {"levels": []}
    tc = p["toolchain"]
    pol = load_conditions(pid)
    levels = []
    for lvl in range(6):
        sel = [k for k, v in TOOLS.items()
               if v["lvl"] == lvl and (not v["langs"] or tc in v["langs"] or tc == "unknown")]
        if FUND_ONLY[0]:
            sel = [k for k in sel if TOOLS[k]["fund"]]
        if lvl == 5:
            sel = sel[:HAMMER_DEPTH]
        levels.append({"level": lvl,
                       "name": ["recon", "static", "symbolic", "dynamic", "adversarial",
                                "hammer"][lvl],
                       "count": len(sel), "tools": sel, "applicable": lvl == 0 or bool(sel),
                       "note": "" if sel else "no applicable methods"})
    return {"levels": levels, "label": tc, "scope_model": pol.get("scope_model", "unknown"),
            "poc_required": bool((pol.get("poc") or {}).get("required")),
            "threshold": THRESHOLD, "details_ok": bool(p["details_ok"]), "web_target": ""}


def state():
    with db() as c:
        rows = c.execute("SELECT * FROM projects ORDER BY rank DESC, id DESC").fetchall()
        hits = {r["pid"]: r["n"] for r in
                c.execute("SELECT pid, COUNT(*) n FROM findings GROUP BY pid").fetchall()}
        unj = {r["pid"]: r["n"] for r in c.execute(
            "SELECT pid, COUNT(*) n FROM findings WHERE judged=0 GROUP BY pid").fetchall()}
        rdy = {r["pid"]: r["n"] for r in c.execute(
            "SELECT pid, COUNT(*) n FROM findings WHERE eligible=1 GROUP BY pid").fetchall()}
        reps = {r["pid"]: r["n"] for r in c.execute(
            "SELECT pid, COUNT(*) n FROM repairs GROUP BY pid").fetchall()}
        tally = {s: 0 for s in SEV_ORDER}
        for r in c.execute("SELECT sev, COUNT(*) n FROM findings GROUP BY sev").fetchall():
            tally[r["sev"]] = r["n"]
    live = running_pids()
    projects = []
    for r in rows:
        d = dict(r)
        d["hits"] = hits.get(r["id"], 0)
        d["unjudged"] = unj.get(r["id"], 0)
        d["ready"] = rdy.get(r["id"], 0)
        d["repairs"] = reps.get(r["id"], 0)
        d["live"] = r["id"] in live
        d["web"] = r["toolchain"] == "web"
        projects.append(d)
    return {"projects": projects, "tally": tally, "running": len(live), "workers": WORKERS,
            "tools": len(TOOLS), "threshold": THRESHOLD, "fund_only": FUND_ONLY[0],
            "stopped": STOP.is_set(), "auto_discover": AUTO_DISCOVER,
            "fund": sum(d["fund_hits"] for d in projects), "repair_kinds": REPAIR_KINDS}


class Bad(Exception):
    def __init__(self, msg, errors=None):
        super().__init__(msg)
        self.errors = errors or [msg]


GET = {}
POST = {}


def get(path):
    def deco(fn):
        GET[path] = fn
        return fn
    return deco


def post(path):
    def deco(fn):
        POST[path] = fn
        return fn
    return deco


@get("/api/state")
def h_state(q):
    return state()


@get("/api/health")
def h_health(q):
    with db() as c:
        t0 = time.time()
        c.execute("SELECT 1").fetchone()
        dbms = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
    with RUN_LOCK:
        live = sorted(RUNNING.keys())
    return {"uptime": int(time.time() - STARTED[0]), "queue": JOBQ.qsize(), "live": live,
            "db_ms": dbms, "net_penalty": round(NET_PENALTY[0], 1),
            "threads": threading.active_count(), "discover": AUTO_DISCOVER,
            "discovering": NET_DISC.is_set(), "workers": WORKERS}

    return [dict(r) for r in rows]

@post("/api/manual")
def p_manual(b):

    if pid:
        errs = manual_update(pid, b)
        if errs:

        return {"id": pid}
    pid, errs = manual_create(b)
    if errs:
        raise Bad(errs[0], errs)
    return {"id": pid}

            c.execute("DELETE FROM log WHERE pid>0")
    return {"ok": True}

                  "cycle=0, readiness=0, cond_ok=0 WHERE id=?", (pid,))
    shutil.rmtree(project_dir(pid), ignore_errors=True)

class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
    server_version = "bountyhunter/10"

    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass

    def _j(self, obj, code=200):
        b = json.dumps(obj, default=str).encode()
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(b)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(b)
        self.close_connection = True

    def _h(self, body):
        b = body.encode()
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(b)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(b)
        self.close_connection = True

    def _body(self):
        n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
        if not n:
            return {}
        try:
            return json.loads(self.rfile.read(n) or b"{}")
        except Exception:
            return {}

    def do_GET(self):
        self.route("GET")

    def do_POST(self):
        self.route("POST")

    def route(self, m):
        u = urlparse(self.path)
        try:
            if u.path in ("/", "/index.html"):
                return self._h(UI.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if m == "GET":
                fn = GET.get(u.path)
                arg = {k: v[0] for k, v in parse_qs(u.query).items()}
            else:
                fn = POST.get(u.path)
                arg = self._body()
            if not fn:
                return self._j({"msg": "not found"}, 404)
            out = fn(arg)
            return self._j(out if out is not None else {"ok": True})
        except Bad as ex:
            return self._j({"msg": str(ex), "errors": ex.errors}, 400)
        except Exception as ex:
            traceback.print_exc()
            return self._j({"msg": "%s: %s" % (type(ex).__name__, ex)}, 500)


def serve():
    for port in PORTS:
        try:
            ThreadingHTTPServer.allow_reuse_address = True
            srv = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), H)
        except OSError:
            continue
        srv.daemon_threads = True
        log(0, "ui: http://127.0.0.1:%d" % port)
        for fn in (log_writer, heartbeat, discover_loop, autopilot):
            threading.Thread(target=fn, daemon=True).start()
        for _ in range(WORKERS):
            threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True).start()
        threading.Thread(target=lambda u: webbrowser.open(u),
                         args=("http://127.0.0.1:%d" % port,), daemon=True).start()
        srv.serve_forever()
        return
    log(0, "no free port")


def main():
    init()
    STOP.clear()
    with db() as c:
        n = c.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM projects").fetchone()["n"]
    log(0, "BountyHunter 10 - %d methods, %d fund, %d projects, %d manual"
        % (len(TOOLS), len(FUND), n, len(manual_rows())))
    log(0, "chain: %d rpc, etherscan %s, eth $%.0f, census %s"
        % (len([v for v in RPC.values() if v]), "set" if ETHERSCAN else "off", ETH_USD,
           "on" if VALUE_CENSUS else "off"))
    log(0, "gates: threshold %d, attempts %d, repairs %d, cycles %d, hammer %d"
        % (THRESHOLD, MAX_ATTEMPTS, MAX_REPAIRS, MAX_CYCLES, HAMMER_DEPTH))
    if DUPES:
        log(0, "env conflicts: %s" % ", ".join(sorted(set(DUPES))))
    serve()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

<script>
"use strict";
const TABS = ["board","overview","value","contract","plan","findings","readiness","report",
              "methods","discovery","repairs","health"];
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const esc = v => (v ?? "").toString().replace(/[&<>"']/g,
  c => ({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c]));
let S = null, SEL = 0, TAB = "board", FILT = "all", TERM = "", FSEV = "all",
    FSORT = {k:"score", d:-1}, SINCE = 0, PAUSE = false, ERRS = false, BUSY = false,
    FAILS = 0, SIGHASH = "", DOCK = true, MPID = 0, TOOLS = null;

function to(m, bad){ const d = document.createElement("div");
  d.className = "to" + (bad ? " bad" : ""); d.textContent = m;
  $("#toast").appendChild(d); setTimeout(() => d.remove(), 3600); }

async function api(p, o){
  const c = new AbortController(); const t = setTimeout(() => c.abort(), 12000);
  try{
    const r = await fetch(p, {...(o||{}), signal:c.signal});
    if (!r.ok){ let j = null; try{ j = await r.json(); }catch{}
      throw new Error((j && (j.msg || (j.errors||[])[0])) || ("HTTP " + r.status)); }
    return await r.json();
  } finally { clearTimeout(t); }
}
const get = p => api(p).catch(() => null);
const post = (p, b) => api(p, {method:"POST", headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json"},
  body:JSON.stringify(b||{})});

async function poll(){
  if (BUSY) return; BUSY = true;
  try{
    const s = await api("/api/state");
    FAILS = 0; $("#lost").classList.remove("on"); $("#dot").classList.remove("bad");
    const h = JSON.stringify([s.tally, s.running, s.fund, s.stopped,
      s.projects.map(p => [p.id,p.status,p.stage,p.attempts,p.escalation,p.readiness,
        p.hits,p.fund,p.ready,p.unjudged,p.manual,p.live])]);
    const ch = h !== SIGHASH; S = s; SIGHASH = h;
    kpi(); rail(); head(); health();
    if (ch) await render();
    await pullLog();
  }catch(e){ if (++FAILS >= 3){ $("#lost").classList.add("on"); $("#dot").classList.add("bad"); } }
  finally{ BUSY = false; }
}

function kpi(){
  const t = (S && S.tally) || {};
  const ready = (S.projects||[]).reduce((a,p) => a + (p.ready||0), 0);
  const avg = S.projects.length ? Math.round(S.projects.reduce((a,p) => a +
    (p.readiness||0), 0) / S.projects.length) : 0;
  $("#k1").textContent = S.projects.length; $("#k2").textContent = S.running;
  $("#k3").textContent = S.fund||0; $("#k4").textContent = t.critical||0;
  $("#k5").textContent = t.high||0; $("#k6").textContent = ready;
  $("#k7").textContent = S.tools; $("#k8").textContent = avg + "%";
  $("#fund").classList.toggle("on", !!S.fund_only);
}
function health(){
  const c = $("#hchip"); if (!S) return;
  const b = [`${S.workers}w`, `${S.tools} methods`, `thr ${S.threshold}`];
  if (S.auto_discover) b.push("discovery");
  if (S.fund_only) b.push("FUND ONLY");
  if (S.stopped) b.push("STOPPED");
  c.textContent = b.join(" · ");
  c.className = S.stopped ? "bad" : (S.fund_only ? "warn" : "");
}
const pill = (t, c) => `<span class="pill ${c||""}">${esc(t)}</span>`;

function row(p){
  const g = [p.manual ? pill("manual","mut") : "",
    p.toolchain === "unknown" ? pill("untyped","bad") : pill(p.toolchain),
    p.details_ok ? "" : pill("no details","warn"),
    p.web ? pill("app","live") : "", p.fund ? pill(p.fund + " fund","cash") : "",
    p.live ? pill("live","live") : "", p.ready ? pill(p.ready + " ready","rdy") : "",
    p.hits ? pill(p.hits + " hit") : "", pill("L" + p.escalation),
    pill(p.status, p.status === "failed" ? "bad" : "")].join("");
  return `<div class="t ${p.id === SEL ? "sel" : ""}" data-id="${p.id}">
    <div class="n">${esc(p.name)}</div><div class="u">${esc(p.slug||"")}</div>
    <div class="b">${g}</div>
    <div class="bar"><i style="width:${Math.min(100,(p.escalation/5)*100)}%"></i></div></div>`;
}
function rail(){
  const box = $("#list"), keep = box.scrollTop, t = TERM.toLowerCase();
  const rows = (S.projects||[]).filter(p => {
    if (t && !(p.name||"").toLowerCase().includes(t) &&
        !(p.url||"").toLowerCase().includes(t)) return false;
    if (FILT === "manual") return p.manual;
    if (FILT === "live") return p.live;
    if (FILT === "cash") return p.fund > 0;
    if (FILT === "ready") return p.ready > 0;
    if (FILT === "hits") return p.hits > 0;
    if (FILT === "nodet") return !p.details_ok;
    if (FILT === "un") return p.toolchain === "unknown";
    if (FILT === "web") return p.web;
    return true;
  });
  box.innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(row).join("") : `<div class="empty">nothing matches</div>`;
  box.scrollTop = keep;
  box.querySelectorAll(".t").forEach(e => e.onclick = () => select(+e.dataset.id));
}
const cur = () => ((S && S.projects) || []).find(p => p.id === SEL) || null;
function select(id){ SEL = id; SINCE = 0; SIGHASH = ""; $("#dlog").innerHTML = ""; render(); }

function head(){
  const p = cur();
  $("#pn").textContent = p ? p.name : "no target selected";
  $("#pu").textContent = p ? p.url : "pick a target on the left, queue one above, or use + manual";
  $("#dn").textContent = p ? p.name : "-";
  const a = $("#acts");
  if (!p){ a.innerHTML = ""; return; }
  a.innerHTML = `<button class="pri" data-a="all">attack all</button>
    <button data-a="recon">recon</button><button data-a="l1">L1</button>
    <button data-a="l2">L2</button><button data-a="l3">L3</button><button data-a="l4">L4</button>
    <button data-a="l5">L5</button><button class="dan" data-a="stop">stop</button>
    <button data-a="restart">restart</button>
    ${p.manual ? '<button class="pri" data-a="edit">edit manual</button>' : ""}
    <select id="rk">${(S.repair_kinds||[]).map(k =>
      `<option value="${esc(k)}">${esc(k)}</option>`).join("")}</select>
    <button data-a="repair">repair</button>
    <button class="gh" data-a="auto">auto ${p.auto ? "on" : "off"}</button>`;
  a.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => act(b.dataset.a, p));
}

async function act(kind, p){
  if (!p) return;
  try{
    if (kind === "all"){ await post("/api/attack", {pid:p.id}); to("full ladder queued"); }
    else if (kind === "stop"){ await post("/api/stop", {pid:p.id}); to("stopping"); }
    else if (kind === "restart"){
      if (!confirm("purge and requeue this target?")) return;
      await post("/api/restart", {pid:p.id}); SINCE = 0; $("#dlog").innerHTML = ""; to("restarted"); }
    else if (kind === "repair"){ await post("/api/repair",
      {pid:p.id, kind:($("#rk")||{}).value||"locked_files"}); to("repair queued"); }
    else if (kind === "auto"){ await post("/api/auto", {pid:p.id, on:!p.auto}); }
    else if (kind === "recon"){ await post("/api/attack", {pid:p.id, level:0}); }
    else if (kind === "edit"){ return openManual(p.id); }
    else { await post("/api/attack", {pid:p.id, level:+kind.slice(1)}); }
    SIGHASH = ""; poll();
  }catch(e){ to(e.message, true); }
}

async function pullLog(){
  if (PAUSE || !SEL) return;
  const rows = await get(`/api/log?pid=${SEL}&since=${SINCE}`);
  if (!rows || !rows.length) return;
  const box = $("#dlog"), bot = box.scrollHeight - box.scrollTop - box.clientHeight < 40;
  const f = ($("#dfilter").value||"").toLowerCase();
  for (const r of rows){
    SINCE = r.id;
    if (f && !r.line.toLowerCase().includes(f)) continue;
    if (ERRS && !/\b(fail|error|failed|crash|timeout|denied|traceback)\b/i.test(r.line)) continue;
    const d = document.createElement("div"); d.className = "ln";
    const c = /\b(fail|error|crash|traceback)\b/i.test(r.line) ? "e"
      : /\b(warn|thin|skip|absent)\b/i.test(r.line) ? "w" : "";
    d.innerHTML = `<span class="ts">${new Date(r.ts*1000).toLocaleTimeString()} </span>` +
      `<span class="${c}">${esc(r.line)}</span>`;
    box.appendChild(d);
  }
  while (box.childElementCount > 1200) box.firstChild.remove();
  if (bot) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
}

function tabs(){
  $("#tabs").innerHTML = TABS.map(t =>
    `<button class="${t === TAB ? "on" : ""}" data-t="${t}">${t}</button>`).join("");
  $("#tabs").querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    TAB = b.dataset.t; tabs(); render(); });
}

async function render(){
  const v = $("#view"), p = cur(); tabs();
  try{
    if (TAB === "board") return board(v);
    if (!p){ v.innerHTML = `<div class="empty">select a target</div>`; return; }
    const map = {overview, value, contract, plan, findings, readiness, report, methods,
                 discovery, repairs, health};
    return await map[TAB](v, p);
  }catch(e){
    v.innerHTML = `<div class="card bad"><b>render failed</b>
      <div class="hint mono">${esc(e.message)}</div>
      <div class="row" style="margin:9px 0 0"><button data-x="r">retry</button></div></div>`;
    v.querySelector("[data-x=r]").onclick = render;
  }
}

function board(v){
  const ps = S.projects || [];
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>all targets · ${ps.length}</h2>
    <p class="hint" style="margin-bottom:10px">manual rows skip the detail gate and keep their
    type locked. everything else is judged the same way.</p>` +
    (ps.length ? `<table><thead><tr><th>target</th><th>source</th><th>details</th>
    <th>type</th><th>money</th><th>status</th><th>ready</th><th>ready %</th><th></th>
    </tr></thead><tbody>` + ps.map(x => `<tr>
      <td><b>${esc(x.name)}</b><div class="u mono">${esc(x.slug)}</div></td>
      <td>${x.manual ? '<span class="chip mut">manual</span>' : '<span class="chip">auto</span>'}</td>
      <td>${x.details_ok ? '<span class="chip ok">yes</span>' : '<span class="chip bad">no</span>'}</td>
      <td>${esc(x.toolchain)}${x.web ? ' <span class="chip ok">app</span>' : ''}</td>
      <td>${x.fund ? `<span class="chip cash">${x.fund}</span>` : "—"}</td>
      <td>${esc(x.status)}</td><td class="num">${x.ready||0}</td>
      <td><span class="sbar"><i style="width:${x.readiness||0}%"></i></span>
        <span class="num hint">${x.readiness||0}%</span></td>
      <td><button data-g="${x.id}">open</button>
        ${x.manual ? `<button data-e="${x.id}">edit</button>` : ""}
        <button class="pri" data-s="${x.id}">start</button></td></tr>`).join("") +
      `</tbody></table>` : `<div class="empty">no targets — queue a url or use + manual</div>`);
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-g]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => select(+b.dataset.g));
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-e]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => openManual(+b.dataset.e));
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-s]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    const p = S.projects.find(z => z.id === +b.dataset.s); if (!p) return;
    SEL = p.id; act("all", p); });
}

async function overview(v, p){
  const d = await get("/api/details?pid=" + p.id) || {};
  v.innerHTML = `
    ${p.manual ? `<div class="card ok"><b>manual project</b>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">details and type are yours. scope, tiers, poc
      and channel still gate every finding.</div>
      <div class="row" style="margin:8px 0 0"><button data-x="edit">edit</button>
      <button data-x="release">release to discovery</button></div></div>` : ""}
    ${!p.details_ok && !p.manual ? `<div class="card warn"><b>details not fetched</b>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">nothing is downloaded until the page and contract
      exist. paste the brief or enter it by hand.</div></div>` : ""}
    ${p.toolchain === "unknown" ? `<div class="card warn"><b>type unresolved</b>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">only language-agnostic methods run.</div></div>` : ""}
    ${p.unjudged ? `<div class="card warn"><b>${p.unjudged} finding(s) never judged</b></div>` : ""}
    <div class="grid">
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.readiness||0}%</b><span>submission ready</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.fund||0}</b><span>fund findings</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.hits}</b><span>total findings</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.targets_n||0}</b><span>in scope</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.repairs}</b><span>repairs</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="card"><b>${esc(p.status)}</b> · stage <span class="mono">${esc(p.stage||"—")}</span>
      · L${p.escalation}/5 · attempt ${p.attempts} · cycle ${p.cycle}
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">rank ${p.rank} · cap $${p.max}
      ${p.tvl ? " · census " + (p.tvl/1e18).toFixed(4) + " ETH-eq" : ""}</div></div>
    ${p.notes ? `<div class="card"><b>notes</b><pre class="rep">${esc(p.notes)}</pre></div>` : ""}
    <div class="card"><b>type: ${esc(p.toolchain)}</b>
      <div class="row" style="margin:9px 0 0">
        <select id="tsel">${["auto","solidity","rust","go","python","js","php","ruby","java","c",
          "web","unknown"].map(t => `<option value="${t}" ${
          (!p.type_locked && t === "auto") || (p.type_locked && t === p.toolchain) ? "selected"
          : ""}>${t}</option>`).join("")}</select>
        <button data-x="type">set type</button></div></div>
    <div class="card"><b>detail resolution</b>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:5px">adapter
        <span class="mono">${esc((d.details||{}).adapter||"none")}</span> ·
        tier <span class="mono">${esc((d.details||{}).tier||"none")}</span></div>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:8px">paste the program text to fill scope, tiers, poc
      and channel.</div>
      <textarea id="paste" rows="6" style="margin-top:6px"></textarea>
      <div class="row" style="margin:7px 0 0"><button class="pri" data-x="paste">parse paste</button>
        <button data-x="recon">retry fetch</button></div>
      <div id="pout" class="hint mono" style="margin-top:7px"></div></div>
    <div class="row"><button class="pri" data-x="all">attack all</button>
      <button data-x="contract">contract</button><button data-x="plan">plan</button>
      <button class="dan" data-x="purge">purge data</button>
      <button data-x="restart">restart</button><button data-x="locked_files">unlock git</button></div>`;
  v.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.onclick = async () => {
    const x = b.dataset.x;
    if (x === "all" || x === "restart") return act(x, p);
    if (x === "edit") return openManual(p.id);
    if (x === "contract" || x === "plan"){ TAB = x; return render(); }
    if (x === "type"){
      await post("/api/type", {pid:p.id, toolchain:$("#tsel").value}).catch(e => to(e.message, true));
      SIGHASH = ""; return poll(); }
    if (x === "release"){
      if (!confirm("drop the manual flag and let discovery own this target?")) return;
      await post("/api/manual/clear", {pid:p.id}); SIGHASH = ""; return poll(); }
    if (x === "paste"){
      const t = $("#paste").value || "";
      if (t.trim().length < 80) return to("paste more text", true);
      try{ const r = await post("/api/paste", {pid:p.id, text:t});
        to(`parsed ${r.assets} asset(s), ${r.tiers} tier(s)`, !r.ok); SIGHASH = ""; poll(); }
      catch(e){ to(e.message, true); }
      return; }
    if (x === "purge"){
      if (!confirm("drop findings, log and contract?")) return;
      await post("/api/purge", {pid:p.id}); SINCE = 0; $("#dlog").innerHTML = "";
      SIGHASH = ""; return poll(); }
    await post("/api/repair", {pid:p.id, kind:x}).catch(e => to(e.message, true));
    to("repair: " + x);
  });
}

async function value(v, p){
  const r = await get("/api/value?pid=" + p.id) || {value:""};
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>value census</h2>
    <div class="grid"><div class="st"><b class="num">${r.tvl ? (r.tvl/1e18).toFixed(4) : "0"}</b>
      <span>eth-equivalent</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${p.targets_n||0}</b><span>in scope</span></div></div>
    <pre class="rep">${esc(r.value || "no census yet")}</pre>
    <div class="row"><button data-x="r">rerun</button></div>`;
  v.querySelector("[data-x=r]").onclick = () => act("recon", p);
}

async function contract(v, p){
  const pol = await get("/api/conditions?pid=" + p.id);
  if (!pol || !p.cond_ok){
    v.innerHTML = `<div class="empty">no contract parsed — run recon, paste, or enter manually</div>`;
    return;
  }
  const ex = pol.exclusions || {}, poc = pol.poc || {}, sub = pol.submit || {};
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>program contract</h2>
    <div class="grid"><div class="st"><b>${esc(pol.scope_model)}</b><span>scope model</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${(pol.targets||[]).length}</b><span>targets</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b>${poc.required ? "required" : "optional"}</b><span>poc</span></div></div>
    <div class="card"><b>targets</b><table><thead><tr><th>address</th><th>chain</th><th>kind</th>
      </tr></thead><tbody>${(pol.targets||[]).map(t => `<tr><td class="mono">${esc(t.address)}</td>
      <td>${esc(t.chain)}</td><td>${esc(t.kind||"—")}</td></tr>`).join("") ||
      `<tr><td colspan="3" class="hint">none</td></tr>`}</tbody></table></div>
    <div class="card"><b>exclusions</b>
      ${(ex.surfaces||[]).map(s => `<div class="chip bad" style="display:block">${esc(s)}</div>`)
        .join("") || '<div class="hint">none</div>'}</div>
    <div class="card"><b>submit</b>
      <div class="hint">channel <b>${esc(sub.channel||"—")}</b> · address
        <span class="mono">${esc(sub.address||"—")}</span></div></div>
    <div class="card"><b>severity tiers</b><table><tbody>
      ${(pol.severity||[]).map(r => `<tr><td class="sev sv-${esc(r.tier)}">${esc(r.tier)}</td>
      <td class="mono">${esc(r.band||"—")}</td></tr>`).join("") ||
      `<tr><td class="hint">none — the gate blocks everything</td></tr>`}</tbody></table></div>
    <div class="card"><b>template</b>
      <div class="hint">${esc((pol.template||[]).join(" / ")||"—")}</div></div>`;
}

async function plan(v, p){
  const pl = await get("/api/plan?pid=" + p.id) || {levels:[]};
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>attack plan</h2>
    <p class="hint" style="margin-bottom:10px">type ${esc(pl.label||"?")} · poc
    ${pl.poc_required ? "required" : "optional"} · threshold ${pl.threshold}</p>` +
    pl.levels.map(l => `<div class="lvl ${l.applicable ? "" : "off"}">
      <div class="n">L${l.level}</div><div class="x"><b>${esc(l.name)} · ${l.count}</b>
      <span>${esc(l.note || (l.tools||[]).slice(0,8).join(", "))}</span></div>
      <button ${l.applicable ? "" : "disabled"} data-l="${l.level}">run</button></div>`).join("");
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-l]").forEach(b => b.onclick = async () => {
    try{ await post("/api/attack", {pid:p.id, level:+b.dataset.l}); to("queued"); poll(); }
    catch(e){ to(e.message, true); } });
}

async function findings(v, p){
  const fs = await get("/api/findings?pid=" + p.id) || [];
  const c = {}; fs.forEach(f => c[f.sev] = (c[f.sev]||0) + 1);
  const fund = fs.filter(f => f.fund).length;
  let shown = FSEV === "all" ? fs : FSEV === "fund" ? fs.filter(f => f.fund)
    : fs.filter(f => f.sev === FSEV);
  const order = "critical high medium low info".split(" ");
  shown = shown.slice().sort((a,b) => {
    const k = FSORT.k;
    const av = k === "sev" ? order.indexOf(a.sev) : (a[k] ?? "");
    const bv = k === "sev" ? order.indexOf(b.sev) : (b[k] ?? "");
    return (av > bv ? 1 : av < bv ? -1 : 0) * FSORT.d; });
  const ch = (id, l, n) => `<button class="${FSEV === id ? "on" : ""}" data-s="${id}">${l} ${n}</button>`;
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>${fs.length} findings</h2>
    <div class="row">${ch("all","all",fs.length)}${fund ? ch("fund","money",fund) : ""}
      ${order.filter(s => c[s]).map(s => ch(s, s, c[s])).join("")}</div>
    ${shown.length ? `<table><thead><tr><th class="s" data-k="sev">sev</th>
      <th class="s" data-k="title">title</th><th>where</th><th class="s" data-k="score">score</th>
      <th>state</th></tr></thead><tbody>` + shown.map(f => {
        let bl = []; try{ bl = JSON.parse(f.blockers||"[]"); }catch{}
        return `<tr class="${f.eligible ? "" : "dead"}">
          <td class="sev sv-${esc(f.sev)}">${esc(f.sev)}</td>
          <td>${f.fund ? '<span class="chip cash">$</span> ' : ""}${esc(f.title)}
            <div class="hint mono">${esc(f.cls)}</div></td>
          <td class="mono">${esc(f.file)}:${f.line}</td>
          <td><span class="sbar"><i style="width:${f.score||0}%"></i></span>
            <span class="num hint">${f.score||0}</span></td>
          <td>${!f.judged ? '<span class="chip">unjudged</span>'
            : bl.length ? bl.map(b => `<span class="chip bad">${esc(b)}</span>`).join("")
            : '<span class="chip ok">ready</span>'}</td></tr>
          <tr><td colspan="5" style="padding-top:0"><details><summary class="hint">evidence
          </summary><pre class="rep">${esc(f.evidence)}</pre></details></td></tr>`; }).join("") +
      `</tbody></table>` : `<div class="empty">no findings in this filter</div>`}`;
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-s]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { FSEV = b.dataset.s; render(); });
  v.querySelectorAll("th.s").forEach(t => t.onclick = () => {
    const k = t.dataset.k; FSORT = {k, d: FSORT.k === k ? -FSORT.d : -1}; render(); });
}

async function readiness(v, p){
  const r = await get("/api/readiness?pid=" + p.id) || {requirements:[]};
  const deg = Math.round((r.score||0) * 3.6);
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>submission readiness</h2>
    <div class="row"><div class="ring"
      style="background:conic-gradient(var(--ok) ${deg}deg,var(--line) ${deg}deg)">
      <i class="num">${r.score||0}%</i></div>
      <div><b class="num">${r.ready||0} ready</b>
      <div class="hint">${r.total||0} total</div></div></div>
    <div class="row"><button class="pri" data-x="poc">generate poc files</button>
      <button data-x="rep">report</button></div>`;
  v.querySelector("[data-x=poc]").onclick = async () => {
    try{ await post("/api/poc", {pid:p.id}); to("written"); poll(); }
    catch(e){ to(e.message, true); } };
  v.querySelector("[data-x=rep]").onclick = () => { TAB = "report"; render(); };
}

async function report(v, p){
  const r = await get("/api/report?pid=" + p.id), txt = (r && r.report) || "";
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>report</h2>
    <div class="row"><button data-x="c">copy</button><button data-x="d">download</button>
      <button class="pri" data-x="l5">rebuild</button></div>
    <pre class="rep">${esc(txt || "not written yet")}</pre>`;
  v.querySelector("[data-x=c]").onclick = () =>
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(() => to("copied"), () => to("copy failed", true));
  v.querySelector("[data-x=d]").onclick = () => {
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([txt], {type:"text/markdown"}));
    a.download = "report-" + (p.slug||p.id) + ".md"; a.click(); };
  v.querySelector("[data-x=l5]").onclick = () => act("l5", p);
}

async function methods(v, p){
  if (!TOOLS) TOOLS = await get("/api/tools") || [];
  const langs = new Set([p.toolchain]);
  const ok = t => t.langs.length === 0 || t.langs.some(l => langs.has(l)) || p.toolchain === "unknown";
  const rows = TOOLS.slice().sort((a,b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>${rows.length} methods · ${TOOLS.filter(t => t.fund).length} fund</h2>
    <table><thead><tr><th>id</th><th>method</th><th>class</th><th>sev</th><th>lvl</th>
    <th></th></tr></thead><tbody>` + rows.map(t => `<tr style="${ok(t) ? "" : "opacity:.35"}">
      <td class="mono">${esc(t.id)}</td>
      <td>${t.fund ? '<span class="chip cash">$</span> ' : ""}${esc(t.name)}</td>
      <td>${esc(t.cls)}</td><td class="sev sv-${esc(t.sev)}">${esc(t.sev)}</td>
      <td>L${t.lvl}</td><td>${ok(t) ? `<button data-t="${esc(t.id)}">run</button>` : ""}</td>
      </tr>`).join("") + `</tbody></table>`;
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-t]").forEach(b => b.onclick = async () => {
    try{ await post("/api/tool", {pid:p.id, id:b.dataset.t}); to(b.dataset.t + " queued"); poll(); }
    catch(e){ to(e.message, true); } });
}

async function discovery(v){
  const cat = await get("/api/catalog") || [];
  const aud = await get("/api/discovery_audit") || [];
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>seed catalog</h2>
    ${cat.map((x,i) => `<div class="card"><b>${esc(x.name)}</b>
      <div class="hint">$${x.max} · hint ${esc(x.toolchain)} · ${esc(x.url)}</div>
      <div class="row" style="margin:7px 0 0"><button data-i="${i}">queue</button></div></div>`
      ).join("") || '<div class="empty">catalog empty</div>'}
    <h2 style="margin-top:16px">rejected urls · ${aud.length}</h2>
    ${aud.length ? `<table><thead><tr><th>url</th><th>reason</th></tr></thead><tbody>
      ${aud.slice(0,120).map(a => `<tr><td class="mono">${esc(a.url)}</td>
      <td class="hint">${esc(a.why)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table>`
      : '<div class="empty">none</div>'}`;
  v.querySelectorAll("[data-i]").forEach(b => b.onclick = async () => {
    const x = cat[+b.dataset.i];
    try{ await post("/api/add", {url:x.url, name:x.name}); to("queued"); poll(); }
    catch(e){ to(e.message, true); } });
}

async function repairs(v){
  const r = await get("/api/repairs") || [];
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>${r.length} repair events</h2>` +
    (r.length ? `<table><thead><tr><th>time</th><th>pid</th><th>kind</th><th>detail</th></tr>
      </thead><tbody>${r.map(x => `<tr><td class="mono">${
      new Date(x.ts*1000).toLocaleTimeString()}</td><td class="num">${x.pid}</td>
      <td>${esc(x.kind)}</td><td class="hint">${esc(x.detail)}</td></tr>`).join("")}
      </tbody></table>` : `<div class="empty">no repairs yet</div>`);
}

async function health(v){
  const h = await get("/api/health") || {};
  v.innerHTML = `<h2>engine health</h2>
    <div class="grid">
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${h.uptime||0}s</b><span>uptime</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${h.queue||0}</b><span>queue depth</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${(h.live||[]).length}</b><span>live jobs</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${h.db_ms||0}ms</b><span>db latency</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${h.net_penalty||0}s</b><span>net penalty</span></div>
      <div class="st"><b class="num">${h.threads||0}</b><span>threads</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="card"><b>flags</b><div class="hint" style="margin-top:6px">
      discovery ${h.discover ? "on" : "off"} · discovering ${h.discovering ? "yes" : "no"} ·
      workers ${h.workers||0} · live ${(h.live||[]).join(", ")||"—"}</div></div>
    <div class="row"><button data-x="r">clear stop flag</button>
      <button class="dan" data-x="s">stop all</button></div>`;
  v.querySelector("[data-x=r]").onclick = () => post("/api/reset").then(() => to("cleared"));
  v.querySelector("[data-x=s]").onclick = () => post("/api/stop", {}).then(() => to("signalled"));
}

async function openManual(pid){
  MPID = pid || 0;
  $("#mtitle").textContent = pid ? "edit manual program" : "manual program";
  $("#merr").textContent = ""; $("#prev").textContent = "nothing parsed yet";
  ["mparse","mname","murl","mmax","mscope","tc","th","tm","tl","ti","maddr","mexcl","mnotes"]
    .forEach(k => $("#" + k).value = "");
  $("#mtype").value = "unknown"; $("#mpoc").value = "1"; $("#mchan").value = "";
  $("#mrank").value = 60;
  if (!pid){
    const ps = await get("/api/manual/presets") || [];
    $("#presets").innerHTML = ps.map(p =>
      `<button data-p="${esc(p.id)}">${esc(p.label)}</button>`).join("");
    $("#presets").querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
      const p = ps.find(x => x.id === b.dataset.p);
      $("#mtype").value = p.toolchain; $("#mpoc").value = p.poc ? "1" : "0";
      $("#mchan").value = p.submit;
      for (const t of p.tiers){
        if (t.tier === "critical") $("#tc").value = t.max;
        if (t.tier === "high") $("#th").value = t.max;
        if (t.tier === "medium") $("#tm").value = t.max;
        if (t.tier === "low") $("#tl").value = t.max;
        if (t.tier === "info") $("#ti").value = t.max;
      }
      preview();
    });
  } else {
    $("#presets").innerHTML = "";
    const d = await get("/api/manual?pid=" + pid) || {};
    $("#mname").value = d.name || ""; $("#murl").value = d.url || "";
    $("#mtype").value = d.toolchain || "unknown"; $("#mmax").value = d.max || "";
    $("#mrank").value = d.rank || 60; $("#mscope").value = (d.scope||[]).join("\n");
    $("#mpoc").value = d.poc ? "1" : "0"; $("#mchan").value = d.channel || "";
    $("#maddr").value = d.address || ""; $("#mexcl").value = (d.exclusions||[]).join("\n");
    $("#mnotes").value = d.notes || "";
    for (const t of d.tiers || []){
      if (t.tier === "critical") $("#tc").value = t.max;
      if (t.tier === "high") $("#th").value = t.max;
      if (t.tier === "medium") $("#tm").value = t.max;
      if (t.tier === "low") $("#tl").value = t.max;
      if (t.tier === "info") $("#ti").value = t.max;
    }
  }
  $("#scrim").classList.add("on");
  $("#mname").focus();
  preview();
}
function closeManual(){ $("#scrim").classList.remove("on"); }

function collect(draft){
  const tiers = [["critical","tc"],["high","th"],["medium","tm"],["low","tl"],["info","ti"]]
    .filter(([, id]) => $("#" + id).value !== "")
    .map(([t, id]) => ({tier:t, max:+$("#" + id).value || 0}));
  return {pid:MPID, name:$("#mname").value.trim(), url:$("#murl").value.trim(),
    toolchain:$("#mtype").value, max:+$("#mmax").value || 0, rank:+$("#mrank").value || 60,
    scope:$("#mscope").value.split("\n").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean),
    tiers, poc:$("#mpoc").value === "1", channel:$("#mchan").value,
    address:$("#maddr").value.trim(),
    exclusions:$("#mexcl").value.split("\n").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean),
    notes:$("#mnotes").value.trim(), draft:!!draft};
}
function preview(){
  const d = collect(false);
  $("#prev").textContent = [`name: ${d.name || "—"}`, `url: ${d.url || "—"}`,
    `type: ${d.toolchain}`, `max: $${d.max}`, `rank: ${d.rank}`,
    `poc: ${d.poc ? "required" : "optional"}`,
    `channel: ${d.channel || "—"} ${d.address}`, `assets: ${d.scope.length}`,
    ...d.scope.slice(0, 6).map(s => "  · " + s),
    `tiers: ${d.tiers.map(t => t.tier + "<=" + t.max).join(", ") || "—"}`,
    `exclusions: ${d.exclusions.length}`].join("\n");
}
["mname","murl","mtype","mmax","mrank","mscope","tc","th","tm","tl","ti","mpoc","mchan",
 "maddr","mexcl","mnotes"].forEach(k => {
  $("#" + k).addEventListener("input", preview);
  $("#" + k).addEventListener("change", preview);
});

selftest.py — encoding-safe
python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def read(p):
    raw = p.read_bytes()
    if raw[:2] in (b"\xff\xfe", b"\xfe\xff"):
        return raw.decode("utf-16", "replace")
    for enc in ("utf-8-sig", "utf-8", "latin-1"):
        try:
            return raw.decode(enc)
        except Exception:
            continue
    return raw.decode("utf-8", "replace")


for f in ("bh.py", "platforms.py", "contract.py", "manual.py", "ui.html"):
    if not (ROOT / f).exists():
        sys.exit("missing: " + f)

if len((ROOT / "ui.html").read_bytes()) < 20000:
    sys.exit("ui.html is only %d bytes — truncated" % (ROOT / "ui.html").stat().st_size)

try:
    import bh
except Exception as ex:
    sys.exit("import failed: %s: %s" % (type(ex).__name__, ex))

bad = []
for n in ("intake", "conditions", "brief", "gate", "report", "poc"):
    if n not in bh.STEP_FN or not callable(bh.STEP_FN[n]):
        bad.append("step " + n)
for k, v in bh.TOOLS.items():
    if not v.get("name") or not v.get("cls"):
        bad.append("tool " + k)
if len(bh.TOOLS) < 100:
    bad.append("tool count %d" % len(bh.TOOLS))
for r in ("/api/state", "/api/manual", "/api/manual/parse", "/api/health", "/api/attack"):
    if r not in read(ROOT / "bh.py"):
        bad.append("route " + r)

ui = read(ROOT / "ui.html")
for n in ("/api/manual", "/api/state", "render", "openManual", "parse into form"):
    if n not in ui:
        bad.append("ui missing " + n)

envf = ROOT / ".env"
if not envf.exists():
    bad.append(".env missing")
else:
    keys = [l.split("=", 1)[0].strip()
            for l in read(envf).splitlines()
            if "=" in l and not l.strip().startswith("#")]
    dup = {k for k in keys if keys.count(k) > 1}
    if dup:
        bad.append("duplicate .env keys: " + ", ".join(sorted(dup)))
    for k in ("ETH_RPC_URL", "RPC_BASE", "ETHERSCAN_KEY", "GITHUB_TOKEN", "BH_THRESHOLD"):
        if k not in keys:
            bad.append(".env missing " + k)

if bad:
    print("SELFTEST FAIL")
    for b in bad:
        print("  -", b)
    sys.exit(1)
print("selftest ok: %d steps, %d methods, %d fund" % (len(bh.STEP_FN), len(bh.TOOLS), len(bh.FUND)))

to:

platforms.py — full replacement
python
"""Sources, detail adapters and the type resolver.

Details use a three step ladder: direct fetch, reader proxy, platform JSON api.
GitHub is resolved through the REST api with the token, never by scraping.
"""
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

UA = "Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; bountyhunter/10)"
READER = "https://r.jina.ai/"

SEEDS = [
    ("tori", "https://docs.tori.finance/security/audits", "solidity", 1000000),
    ("aladdin", "https://docs.aladdin.club/security-bounty", "solidity", 500000),
    ("rigoblock", "https://docs.rigoblock.com/bug-bounty", "solidity", 10000),
    ("hackenproof", "https://hackenproof.com/programs", "web", 50000),
    ("immunefi", "https://immunefi.com/bug-bounty/", "solidity", 1000000),
    ("bugcrowd", "https://bugcrowd.com/programs", "web", 30000),
    ("cantina", "https://cantina.xyz/competitions", "solidity", 200000),
    ("sherlock", "https://audits.sherlock.xyz/contests", "solidity", 100000),
    ("code4rena", "https://code4rena.com/audits", "solidity", 100000),
    ("hats", "https://hats.finance/", "solidity", 50000),
    ("huntr", "https://huntr.com/bounties", "python", 5000),
    ("intigriti", "https://www.intigriti.com/programs", "web", 20000),
]

SKIP = {"programs", "bounties", "contests", "audits", "program", "all", "new", "index"}
LANG = {"solidity": "solidity", "rust": "rust", "go": "go", "python": "python",
        "javascript": "js", "typescript": "js", "php": "php", "ruby": "ruby",
        "java": "java", "c": "c", "c++": "c"}


def _host(url):
    return re.sub(r"^https?://", "", url).split("/")[0]


def _get(url, timeout=30):
    import urllib.request
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": UA, "Accept": "*/*"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
        return r.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")


def collect_all():
    progs, audits, pins, rej = [], [], [], []
    seen = set()
    for name, url, tc, mx in SEEDS:
        try:
            body = _get(url)
        except Exception as ex:
            rej.append((url, str(ex)[:80]))
            continue
        if not body:
            rej.append((url, "empty body"))
            continue
        found = 0
        for m in re.finditer(r'href="(/(?:programs|program|bounties|contests|audits)/[\w./-]+)"',
                             body):
            path = m.group(1).rstrip("/")
            slug = path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
            if len(slug) < 3 or slug.lower() in SKIP:
                continue
            u = "https://" + _host(url) + path
            if u in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(u)
            progs.append((name + "-" + slug[:24], u, tc, mx))
            found += 1
            if found >= 6:
                break
        if not found:
            audits.append((name, url))
    return progs, audits, pins, rej, [], [], []


def _github(owner, repo):
    import bh
    j = json.loads(bh.gh("/repos/%s/%s" % (owner, repo)))
    lines = ["# %s/%s" % (owner, repo), "", j.get("description") or "", "",
             "language: %s" % (j.get("language") or "unknown"),
             "default_branch: %s" % (j.get("default_branch") or "main")]
    try:
        langs = json.loads(bh.gh("/repos/%s/%s/languages" % (owner, repo)))
        lines.append("languages: %s" % ", ".join(langs))
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        tree = json.loads(bh.gh("/repos/%s/%s/git/trees/%s?recursive=1"
                                % (owner, repo, j.get("default_branch") or "main")))
        paths = [t["path"] for t in tree.get("tree", []) if t.get("type") == "blob"][:500]
        lines += ["", "files:"] + paths
    except Exception:
        pass
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _direct(url):
    m = re.match(r"https?://(?:www\.)?github\.com/([\w.-]+)/([\w.-]+)", url)
    if m:
        return _github(m.group(1), m.group(2).rstrip("/."))
    return _get(url)


def _reader(url):
    return _get(READER + url, timeout=60)


def _api(url):
    m = re.search(r"hackenproof\.com/programs?/([\w-]+)", url)
    if m:
        return _get("https://hackenproof.com/api/programs/%s" % m.group(1))
    m = re.search(r"immunefi\.com/(?:bug-bounty|bounty)/([\w-]+)", url)
    if m:
        return _get("https://immunefi.com/bounty/%s/" % m.group(1))
    m = re.search(r"cantina\.xyz/(?:competitions|audits)/([\w-]+)", url)
    if m:
        return _get("https://cantina.xyz/api/competitions/%s" % m.group(1))
    raise RuntimeError("no api adapter")


def details(url, d):
    """Ladder: direct, reader proxy, platform api. Raises with the last reason."""
    last = "no attempt"
    for name, fn in (("direct", _direct), ("reader", _reader), ("api", _api)):
        try:
            body = fn(url)
        except Exception as ex:
            last = "%s: %s" % (name, str(ex)[:90])
            continue
        if not body or len(body) < 200:
            last = "%s: thin (%d bytes)" % (name, len(body or ""))
            continue
        (Path(d) / "details.html").write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
        (Path(d) / "details.json").write_text(
            json.dumps({"url": url, "bytes": len(body), "adapter": name, "tier": name,
                        "reason": ""}), encoding="utf-8")
        return True
    (Path(d) / "details.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({"url": url, "bytes": 0, "adapter": "none", "tier": "none",
                    "reason": last}), encoding="utf-8")
    raise RuntimeError(last)


def resolve_type(d, url):
    p = Path(d)
    for name, tc in (("Cargo.toml", "rust"), ("foundry.toml", "solidity"),
                     ("hardhat.config.js", "solidity"), ("go.mod", "go"),
                     ("requirements.txt", "python"), ("composer.json", "php"),
                     ("Gemfile", "ruby"), ("pom.xml", "java"), ("package.json", "js")):
        if (p / name).exists():
            return tc, "manifest", {"reason": name, "manifest": [name]}
    html = p / "details.html"
    if html.exists():
        text = html.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").lower()
        m = re.search(r"languages?:\s*([a-z+#, ]+)", text)
        if m:
            for word in re.split(r"[,\s]+", m.group(1)):
                if word in LANG:
                    return LANG[word], "github language", {"reason": "language " + word}
        m = re.search(r"language:\s*([a-z+#]+)", text)
        if m and m.group(1) in LANG:
            return LANG[m.group(1)], "github language", {"reason": "language " + m.group(1)}
    if any(p.glob("**/*.sol")):
        return "solidity", "census", {"reason": "sol files"}
    if "web" in url:
        return "web", "address", {"reason": "web url"}
    return "unknown", "unresolved", {"reason": "no evidence"}

After this, python reset.py once so the tvl column type updates, then selftest.py and run.bat.

What changes on the stuck rows: hackenproof-at and hackenproof-shapeShift will now go direct → reader → api, and the intake line will record which tier answered or why all three failed, instead of a bare details failed. A github.com/org/repo target stops scraping HTML entirely and goes through /repos/… plus /languages with your token, so the type resolver gets a real language and the census gets real paths.

On Tori Finance: type unknown alongside 17 in-scope addresses is expected — the audits page lists contracts but ships no manifest and no repo, so nothing can confirm a toolchain. Set the type manually in the overview card (solidity) and it locks; the language-agnostic methods already running will then be joined by the Solidity set.

give ui full updated code with bigger fonts
